===== M0007 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1802-1837
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1802 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1803 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1804 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1805 |     }
1806 | 
1807 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1808 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1809 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1810 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1811 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1812 |       skipEOLs();
1813 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1814 |         next();
1815 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1816 |       }
1817 |     }
1818 | 
1819 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1820 | 
1821 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1822 |       next();
1823 |       skipEOLs();
1824 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1825 |       if (memberType != null) {
1826 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1827 | 
1828 |         skipEOLs();
1829 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1830 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1831 |         }
1832 | 
1833 |         next();
1834 |       }
1835 |     }
1836 |     return typeNameNode;
1837 |   }

===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1582-1651
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1582 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1583 |     FunctionType functionType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(n.getJSType());
1584 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1585 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1586 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1587 |       if (baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1588 |           baseConstructor != null &&
1589 |           baseConstructor.isInterface()) {
1590 |         compiler.report(
1591 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1592 |                         "constructor", functionPrivateName));
1593 |       } else {
1594 |         if (baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE)) {
1595 |           ObjectType proto = functionType.getPrototype();
1596 |           if (functionType.makesStructs() && !proto.isStruct()) {
1597 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1598 |                                         "struct", functionPrivateName));
1599 |           } else if (functionType.makesDicts() && !proto.isDict()) {
1600 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1601 |                                         "dict", functionPrivateName));
1602 |           }
1603 |         }
1604 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1605 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1606 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1607 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1608 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1609 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1610 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1611 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1612 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1613 |               badImplementedType = true;
1614 |             }
1615 |           } else {
1616 |             badImplementedType = true;
1617 |           }
1618 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1619 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1620 |           }
1621 |         }
1622 |         // check properties
1623 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1624 |       }
1625 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1626 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1627 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1628 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1629 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1630 |           compiler.report(
1631 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1632 |                           "interface", functionPrivateName));
1633 |         }
1634 |       }
1635 | 
1636 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1637 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1638 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1639 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1640 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1641 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1642 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1643 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1644 |           currentProperties.clear();
1645 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1646 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1647 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1648 |         }
1649 |       }
1650 |     }
1651 |   }

===== M0029 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyAccess(JSType,String,NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1392-1420
Documentation:
/**
   * Emit a warning if we can prove that a property cannot possibly be
   * defined on an object. Note the difference between JS and a strictly
   * statically typed language: we're checking if the property
   * *cannot be defined*, whereas a java compiler would check if the
   * property *can be undefined*.
   */
Method code:
1392 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1393 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1394 |     // If the property type is unknown, check the object type to see if it
1395 |     // can ever be defined. We explicitly exclude CHECKED_UNKNOWN (for
1396 |     // properties where we've checked that it exists, or for properties on
1397 |     // objects that aren't in this binary).
1398 |     JSType propType = getJSType(n);
1399 |     if (propType.isEquivalentTo(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1400 |       childType = childType.autobox();
1401 |       ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(childType);
1402 |       if (objectType != null) {
1403 |         // We special-case object types so that checks on enums can be
1404 |         // much stricter, and so that we can use hasProperty (which is much
1405 |         // faster in most cases).
1406 |         if (!objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
1407 |             objectType.isEquivalentTo(
1408 |                 typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1409 |           if (objectType instanceof EnumType) {
1410 |             report(t, n, INEXISTENT_ENUM_ELEMENT, propName);
1411 |           } else {
1412 |             checkPropertyAccessHelper(objectType, propName, t, n);
1413 |           }
1414 |         }
1415 | 
1416 |       } else {
1417 |         checkPropertyAccessHelper(childType, propName, t, n);
1418 |       }
1419 |     }
1420 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

===== M0035 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1484-1591
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1484 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1485 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1486 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1487 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1488 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1489 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1490 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1491 | 
1492 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1493 |       // 1) @type annotation / @enum annotation
1494 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1495 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1496 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1497 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1498 |       //
1499 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1500 |       // the function has JsDoc or has not been declared before.
1501 |       //
1502 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1503 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1504 | 
1505 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1506 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1507 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1508 |         // Determining type for #5
1509 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1510 |       }
1511 | 
1512 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1513 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1514 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1515 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1516 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1517 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1518 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1519 |         if (qVar != null) {
1520 |           // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1521 |           // and they assign F.prototype to an object literal,
1522 |           // then they are responsible for making sure that the object literal's
1523 |           // implicit prototype is set up appropriately. We just obey
1524 |           // the @extends tag.
1525 |           ObjectType qVarType = ObjectType.cast(qVar.getType());
1526 |           if (qVarType != null &&
1527 |               rhsValue != null &&
1528 |               rhsValue.isObjectLit()) {
1529 |             typeRegistry.resetImplicitPrototype(
1530 |                 rhsValue.getJSType(), qVarType.getImplicitPrototype());
1531 |           } else if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1532 |             // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1533 |             // and they assign F.prototype to some arbitrary expression,
1534 |             // there's not much we can do. We just ignore the expression,
1535 |             // and hope they've annotated their code in a way to tell us
1536 |             // what props are going to be on that prototype.
1537 |             return;
1538 |           }
1539 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1540 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1541 |           }
1542 |         }
1543 |       }
1544 | 
1545 |       if (valueType == null) {
1546 |         if (parent.isExprResult()) {
1547 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1548 |               n,
1549 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1550 |               ownerName));
1551 |         }
1552 | 
1553 |         return;
1554 |       }
1555 | 
1556 |       boolean inferred = isQualifiedNameInferred(
1557 |           qName, n, info, rhsValue, valueType);
1558 |       if (!inferred) {
1559 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1560 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1561 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1562 |           // declared yet.
1563 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1564 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1565 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1566 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1567 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1568 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1569 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, n);
1570 |           }
1571 |         }
1572 | 
1573 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1574 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1575 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1576 |       } else if (rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isTrue()) {
1577 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1578 |         FunctionType ownerType =
1579 |             JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(getObjectSlot(ownerName));
1580 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1581 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ownerType.getTypeOfThis();
1582 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1583 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1584 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1585 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1586 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1587 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE), true);
1588 |           }
1589 |         }
1590 |       }
1591 |     }

/**
   * Main entry point for this phase of processing. This follows the pattern for
   * JSCompiler phases.
   *
   * @param externsRoot The root of the externs parse tree.
   * @param jsRoot The root of the input parse tree to be checked.

355 |   @Override

===== M0058 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 158-174
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
158 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
159 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
160 | 
161 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
162 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
163 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
164 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
165 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
166 |       }
167 | 
168 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
169 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
170 |       if (first.isName() &&
171 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
172 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
173 |       }
174 |     }

===== M0061 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 182-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
182 |   boolean parse() {
183 |     int lineno;
184 |     int charno;
185 | 
186 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
187 |     JSTypeExpression type;
188 | 
189 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
190 |     skipEOLs();
191 | 
192 |     JsDocToken token = next();
193 | 
194 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
195 | 
196 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
197 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
198 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
199 |       token = blockInfo.token;
200 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
201 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
202 |       }
203 |     } else {
204 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
205 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
206 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
207 |         // what it was.
208 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
213 |     retry: for (;;) {
214 |       switch (token) {
215 |         case ANNOTATION:
216 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
217 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
218 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
219 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
220 | 
221 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
222 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
223 |             if (annotation == null) {
224 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
225 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |             } else {
227 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
228 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
229 | 
230 |               switch (annotation) {
231 |                 case AUTHOR:
232 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
233 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
234 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
235 | 
236 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
237 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
238 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                     } else {
240 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
241 |                     }
242 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
243 |                   } else {
244 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
245 |                   }
246 |                   continue retry;
247 | 
248 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
249 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
250 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
251 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case STRUCT:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordStruct()) {
258 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
259 |                                           stream.getLineno(),
260 |                                           stream.getCharno());
261 |                   }
262 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
263 |                   continue retry;
264 | 
265 |                 case DICT:
266 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDict()) {
267 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
268 |                                           stream.getLineno(),
269 |                                           stream.getCharno());
270 |                   }
271 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
272 |                   continue retry;
273 | 
274 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
275 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
276 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
277 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
278 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
279 |                     } else {
280 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
281 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
282 |                     }
283 |                   }
284 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
285 |                   continue retry;
286 | 
287 |                 case DEPRECATED:
288 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
289 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
290 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
291 |                   }
292 | 
293 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
294 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
295 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
296 | 
297 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
298 | 
299 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
300 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
301 |                   }
302 | 
303 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
304 |                   continue retry;
305 | 
306 |                 case INTERFACE:
307 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
308 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
309 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
310 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
311 |                     } else {
312 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
313 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
314 |                     }
315 |                   }
316 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
317 |                   continue retry;
318 | 
319 |                 case DESC:
320 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
321 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
322 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
323 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
324 |                     continue retry;
325 |                   } else {
326 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
327 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
328 | 
329 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
330 | 
331 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
332 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
333 |                     continue retry;
334 |                   }
335 | 
336 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
337 |                   String fileOverview = "";
338 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
339 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
340 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
341 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
342 | 
343 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
344 | 
345 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
346 |                   } else {
347 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
348 |                   }
349 | 
350 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview)) {
351 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
352 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
353 |                   }
354 |                   continue retry;
355 | 
356 |                 case LICENSE:
357 |                 case PRESERVE:
358 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
359 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
360 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
361 | 
362 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
363 | 
364 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
365 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
366 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
367 |                     }
368 |                   }
369 | 
370 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
371 |                   continue retry;
372 | 
373 |                 case ENUM:
374 |                   token = next();
375 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
376 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
377 | 
378 |                   type = null;
379 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
380 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
381 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
382 |                   }
383 | 
384 |                   if (type == null) {
385 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
386 |                   }
387 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
388 |                     parser.addTypeWarning(
389 |                         "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
390 |                   }
391 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
392 |                   continue retry;
393 | 
394 |                 case EXPORT:
395 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
396 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
397 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
398 |                   }
399 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
400 |                   continue retry;
401 | 
402 |                 case EXPOSE:
403 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExpose()) {
404 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.expose",
405 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
406 |                   }
407 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
408 |                   continue retry;
409 | 
410 |                 case EXTERNS:
411 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
412 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
413 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
414 |                   }
415 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
416 |                   continue retry;
417 | 
418 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
419 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
420 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
421 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
422 |                   }
423 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
424 |                   continue retry;
425 | 
426 |                 case EXTENDS:
427 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
428 |                   skipEOLs();
429 |                   token = next();
430 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
431 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
432 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
433 | 
434 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
435 |                     token = next();
436 |                     matchingRc = true;
437 |                   }
438 | 
439 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
440 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
441 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
442 | 
443 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
444 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
445 | 
446 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
447 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
448 | 
449 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
450 |                       // record the extended type, check later
451 |                       extendedTypes.add(new ExtendedTypeInfo(
452 |                           type, stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno()));
453 |                     } else {
454 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
455 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
456 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
457 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
458 |                             lineno, charno);
459 |                       }
460 |                     }
461 |                     token = next();
462 |                     if (matchingRc) {
463 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
464 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
465 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
466 |                       }
467 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
468 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
469 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
470 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
471 |                     }
472 |                   } else {
473 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
474 |                   }
475 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
476 |                   continue retry;
477 | 
478 |                 case HIDDEN:
479 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
480 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
481 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
482 |                   }
483 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
484 |                   continue retry;
485 | 
486 |                 case LENDS:
487 |                   skipEOLs();
488 | 
489 |                   matchingRc = false;
490 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
491 |                     token = next();
492 |                     matchingRc = true;
493 |                   }
494 | 
495 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
496 |                     token = next();
497 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordLends(stream.getString())) {
498 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.incompatible",
499 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
500 |                     }
501 |                   } else {
502 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.missing",
503 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
504 |                   }
505 | 
506 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
507 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
508 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
509 |                   }
510 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
511 |                   continue retry;
512 | 
513 |                 case MEANING:
514 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
515 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
516 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
517 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
518 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
519 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
520 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
521 |                   }
522 |                   continue retry;
523 | 
524 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
525 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
526 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
527 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
528 |                   }
529 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
530 |                   continue retry;
531 | 
532 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
533 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
534 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
535 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
536 |                   }
537 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
538 |                   continue retry;
539 | 
540 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
541 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
542 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
543 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
544 |                   }

547 |

549 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
550 |                   continue retry;
551 | 
552 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
553 |                 case OVERRIDE:
554 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
555 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
556 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
557 |                   }

561 |                 case THROWS:
562 |                   skipEOLs();

567 | 
568 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
569 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
570 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
571 | 
572 |                     if (type == null) {
573 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
574 |                       // recovering parsing
575 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
576 |                       continue retry;
577 |                     }
578 |                   }
579 | 
580 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
581 |                   token = current();

586 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).

588 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
589 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);

594 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
595 |                     }

597 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
598 |                   } else {
599 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
600 |                   }
601 |                   continue retry;
602 | 
603 |                 case PARAM:
604 |                   skipEOLs();
605 |                   token = next();
606 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
607 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
608 |                   type = null;
609 | 
610 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
611 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
612 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));

614 |                     if (type == null) {
615 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
616 |                       // recovering parsing
617 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
618 |                       continue retry;
619 |                     }
620 |                     skipEOLs();
621 |                     token = next();
622 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
623 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
624 |                   }
625 | 
626 |                   String name = null;
627 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
628 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
629 |                     token = next();
630 |                   }
631 | 
632 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
633 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
634 |                         lineno, charno);
635 |                   } else {
636 |                     name = stream.getString();
637 | 
638 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
639 |                       token = next();
640 | 
641 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter
642 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
643 |                       // system.
644 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
645 |                         token = next();
646 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
647 |                           token = next();
648 |                         }
649 |                       }
650 | 
651 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
652 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
653 |                       } else if (type != null) {
654 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
655 |                         // already.
656 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
657 |                       }
658 |                     }
659 | 
660 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
661 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
662 |                     // for handling properties of params.
663 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
664 |                       name = null;
665 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
666 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
667 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
668 |                             lineno, charno);
669 |                       } else {
670 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
671 |                             lineno, charno);
672 |                       }
673 |                     }
674 |                   }
675 | 
676 |                   if (name == null) {
677 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
678 |                     continue retry;
679 |                   }
680 | 
681 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, sourceFile, lineno, charno);
682 | 
683 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
684 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
685 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
686 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
687 | 
688 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
689 | 
690 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
691 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
692 |                           paramDescription);
693 |                     }
694 | 
695 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
696 |                   } else {
697 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
698 |                   }
699 |                   continue retry;
700 | 
701 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
702 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
703 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
704 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
705 |                   }
706 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
707 |                   continue retry;
708 | 
709 |                 case NO_SHADOW:

711 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
712 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
713 |                   }

715 |                   continue retry;
716 |

718 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {

720 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
721 |                   }
722 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
723 |                   continue retry;
724 | 
725 |                 case MODIFIES:
726 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
727 |                   continue retry;
728 | 
729 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:

731 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
732 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
733 |                   }

735 |                   continue retry;
736 | 
737 |                 case SEE:
738 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
739 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
740 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
741 |

743 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
744 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

746 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);

752 |                   }

757 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.stableidgen",
758 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
759 |                   }
760 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
761 |                   continue retry;
762 | 
763 |                 case SUPPRESS:
764 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
765 |                   continue retry;
766 | 
767 |                 case TEMPLATE:
768 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();

770 |                       Splitter.on(',')
771 |                           .trimResults()
772 |                           .split(templateInfo.string));

774 |                   if (names.size() == 0 || names.get(0).length() == 0) {

804 |                     }

818 |                 case TYPEDEF:
819 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
820 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();

822 |                   Node typeNode = null;
823 |                   boolean hasType = lookAheadForTypeAnnotation();
824 |                   boolean isAlternateTypeAnnotation =
825 |                       (annotation == Annotation.PRIVATE ||
826 |                        annotation == Annotation.PROTECTED ||
827 |                        annotation == Annotation.PUBLIC ||
828 |                        annotation == Annotation.CONSTANT);
829 |                   boolean canSkipTypeAnnotation =
830 |                       (isAlternateTypeAnnotation ||
831 |                        annotation == Annotation.RETURN);
832 |                   type = null;
833 |                   if (hasType || !canSkipTypeAnnotation) {
834 |                     skipEOLs();
835 |                     token = next();
836 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
837 | 
838 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
839 |                       typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
840 |                     }

842 |                   }
843 | 
844 |                   // The error was reported during recursive descent
845 |                   // recovering parsing
846 |                   boolean hasError = type == null && !canSkipTypeAnnotation;
847 |                   if (!hasError) {
848 |                     // Record types for @type.
849 |                     // If the @private, @protected, or @public annotations
850 |                     // have a type attached, pretend that they actually wrote:
851 |                     // @type {type}\n@private
852 |                     // This will have some weird behavior in some cases
853 |                     // (for example, @private can now be used as a type-cast),
854 |                     // but should be mostly OK.
855 |                     if ((type != null && isAlternateTypeAnnotation)
856 |                         || annotation == Annotation.TYPE) {
857 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
858 |                         parser.addTypeWarning(
859 |                             "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
860 |                       }
861 |                     }
862 | 
863 |                     switch (annotation) {
864 |                       case CONSTANT:
865 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
866 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
867 |                               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
868 |                         }
869 |                         break;
870 | 
871 |                       case DEFINE:
872 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
873 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
874 |                               lineno, charno);
875 |                         }
876 |                         break;
877 | 
878 |                       case PRIVATE:
879 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
880 |                           parser.addParserWarning(
881 |                               "msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
882 |                               lineno, charno);
883 |                         }
884 |                         break;
885 | 
886 |                       case PROTECTED:
887 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
888 |                           parser.addParserWarning(
889 |                               "msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
890 |                               lineno, charno);
891 |                         }
892 |                         break;
893 | 
894 |                       case PUBLIC:
895 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
896 |                           parser.addParserWarning(
897 |                               "msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
898 |                               lineno, charno);
899 |                         }
900 |                         break;
901 | 
902 |                       case RETURN:
903 |                         if (type == null) {
904 |                           type = createJSTypeExpression(newNode(Token.QMARK));
905 |                         }
906 | 
907 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
908 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
909 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
910 |                           break;
911 |                         }
912 | 
913 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
914 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
915 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
916 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
917 | 
918 |                           String returnDescription =
919 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
920 | 
921 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
922 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
923 |                                 returnDescription);
924 |                           }
925 | 
926 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
927 |                         } else {
928 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
929 |                         }
930 |                         continue retry;
931 | 
932 |                       case THIS:
933 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
934 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
935 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
936 |                         }
937 |                         break;
938 | 
939 |                       case TYPEDEF:
940 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
941 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
942 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
943 |                         }
944 |                         break;
945 |                     }
946 |                   }
947 | 
948 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
949 |                   continue retry;
950 |               }
951 |             }
952 |           }
953 |           break;
954 | 
955 |         case EOC:
956 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
957 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
958 |           }
959 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
960 |           return true;
961 | 
962 |         case EOF:
963 |           // discard any accumulated information
964 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
965 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
966 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
967 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
968 |           return false;
969 | 
970 |         case EOL:
971 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
972 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
973 |           }
974 |           token = next();
975 |           continue retry;
976 | 
977 |         default:
978 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
979 |             token = next();
980 |             continue retry;
981 |           } else {
982 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
983 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
984 |             continue retry;
985 |           }
986 |       }
987 | 
988 |       // next token
989 |       token = next();
990 |     }
991 |   }

===== M0064 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 624-636
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
624 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
625 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
626 |     if (typeSet == null) {
627 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
628 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
632 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
633 | 
634 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
635 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
636 |   }

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 963-970
Documentation:
/**
   * We only do forward type inference. We do not do full backwards
   * type inference.
   *
   * In other words, if we have,
   * <code>
   * var x = f();
   * g(x);
   * </code>
   * a forward type-inference engine would try to figure out the type
   * of "x" from the return type of "f". A backwards type-inference engine
   * would try to figure out the type of "x" from the parameter type of "g".
   *
   * However, there are a few special syntactic forms where we do some
   * some half-assed backwards type-inference, because programmers
   * expect it in this day and age. To take an example from Java,
   * <code>
   * List<String> x = Lists.newArrayList();
   * </code>
   * The Java compiler will be able to infer the generic type of the List
   * returned by newArrayList().
   *
   * In much the same way, we do some special-case backwards inference for
   * JS. Those cases are enumerated here.
   */
Method code:
963 |   private void backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
964 |     boolean updatedFnType = inferTemplatedTypesForCall(n, fnType);
965 |     if (updatedFnType) {
966 |       fnType = n.getFirstChild().getJSType().toMaybeFunctionType();
967 |     }
968 |     updateTypeOfParameters(n, fnType);
969 |     updateBind(n, fnType);
970 |   }

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 582-644
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
582 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
583 |     if (returnType == null) {
584 |       // Infer return types.
585 |       // We need to be extremely conservative about this, because of two
586 |       // competing needs.
587 |       // 1) If we infer the return type of f too widely, then we won't be able
588 |       //    to assign f to other functions.
589 |       // 2) If we infer the return type of f too narrowly, then we won't be
590 |       //    able to override f in subclasses.
591 |       // So we only infer in cases where the user doesn't expect to write
592 |       // @return annotations--when it's very obvious that the function returns
593 |       // nothing.
594 |       if (!contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() &&
595 |           !contents.mayHaveSingleThrow() &&
596 |           !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
597 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
598 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     if (returnType == null) {
603 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
607 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
608 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
609 |     }
610 | 
611 |     FunctionType fnType;
612 |     if (isConstructor) {
613 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
614 |     } else if (isInterface) {
615 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
616 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode());
617 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
618 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
619 |       }
620 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
621 |     } else {
622 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
623 |           .withName(fnName)
624 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
625 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
626 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
627 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
628 |           .withTemplateKeys(templateTypeNames)
629 |           .build();
630 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
631 |     }
632 | 
633 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
634 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
638 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
639 |     }
640 | 
641 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeNames();
642 | 
643 |     return fnType;
644 |   }

/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
1015 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {
1016 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
1017 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
1018 |       return that.toMaybeUnionType().getLeastSupertype(this);
1019 |     }
1020 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
1021 |   }

203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {

===== M0111 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.inferArguments(Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 121-164
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers all of a function's arguments if their types aren't declared.
   */
Method code:
121 |   private void inferArguments(Scope functionScope) {
122 |     Node functionNode = functionScope.getRootNode();
123 |     Node astParameters = functionNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
124 |     Node iifeArgumentNode = null;
125 | 
126 |     if (NodeUtil.isCallOrNewTarget(functionNode)) {
127 |       iifeArgumentNode = functionNode.getNext();
128 |     }
129 | 
130 |     FunctionType functionType =
131 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(functionNode.getJSType());
132 |     if (functionType != null) {
133 |       Node parameterTypes = functionType.getParametersNode();
134 |       if (parameterTypes != null) {
135 |         Node parameterTypeNode = parameterTypes.getFirstChild();
136 |         for (Node astParameter : astParameters.children()) {
137 |           Var var = functionScope.getVar(astParameter.getString());
138 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(var);
139 |           if (var.isTypeInferred() &&
140 |               var.getType() == unknownType) {
141 |             JSType newType = null;
142 | 
143 |             if (iifeArgumentNode != null) {
144 |               newType = iifeArgumentNode.getJSType();
145 |             } else if (parameterTypeNode != null) {
146 |               newType = parameterTypeNode.getJSType();
147 |             }
148 | 
149 |             if (newType != null) {
150 |               var.setType(newType);
151 |               astParameter.setJSType(newType);
152 |             }
153 |           }
154 | 
155 |           if (parameterTypeNode != null) {
156 |             parameterTypeNode = parameterTypeNode.getNext();
157 |           }
158 |           if (iifeArgumentNode != null) {
159 |             iifeArgumentNode = iifeArgumentNode.getNext();
160 |           }
161 |         }

===== M0119 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1937-1939
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1937 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1938 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1939 |   }

   * Returns true iff {@code this} can be a {@code dict}.

322 |   public boolean isDict() {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodingConventions.DefaultCodingConvention.describeFunctionBind(Node,boolean)

Original range: 423-463
Documentation:

Method code:
423 |     @Override
424 |     public Bind describeFunctionBind(Node n, boolean useTypeInfo) {
425 |       if (!n.isCall()) {
426 |         return null;
427 |       }
428 | 
429 |       Node callTarget = n.getFirstChild();
430 |       String name = callTarget.getQualifiedName();
431 |       if (name != null) {
432 |         if (name.equals("Function.prototype.bind.call")) {
433 |           // goog.bind(fn, self, args...);
434 |           Node fn = callTarget.getNext();
435 |           if (fn == null) {
436 |             return null;
437 |           }
438 |           Node thisValue = safeNext(fn);
439 |           Node parameters = safeNext(thisValue);
440 |           return new Bind(fn, thisValue, parameters);
441 |         }
442 |       }
443 | 
444 |       if (callTarget.isGetProp()
445 |           && callTarget.getLastChild().getString().equals("bind")) {
446 |         Node maybeFn = callTarget.getFirstChild();
447 |         JSType maybeFnType = maybeFn.getJSType();
448 |         FunctionType fnType = null;
449 |         if (useTypeInfo && maybeFnType != null) {
450 |           fnType = maybeFnType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined()
451 |               .toMaybeFunctionType();
452 |         }
453 | 
454 |         if (fnType != null || maybeFn.isFunction()) {
455 |           // (function(){}).bind(self, args...);
456 |           Node thisValue = callTarget.getNext();
457 |           Node parameters = safeNext(thisValue);
458 |           return new Bind(maybeFn, thisValue, parameters);
459 |         }
460 |       }
461 | 
462 |       return null;
463 |     }

/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
3066 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
3067 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
3068 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = isFunctionDeclaration(n);
3069 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {

===== M0136 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 511-546
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
514 | 
515 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
516 | 
517 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
518 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
519 | 
520 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
521 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
522 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     curNode = n;
526 |     pushScope(n);
527 | 
528 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
529 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
530 |       // scope.
531 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
535 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
536 | 
537 |     // Args
538 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
539 | 
540 |     // Body
541 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
542 |             body.isBlock(), body);
543 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
544 | 
545 |     popScope();
546 |   }

/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
596 |   public Scope getScope() {
597 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
598 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
599 |       return scope;
600 |     }
601 |

603 |     while (it.hasNext()) {

608 |

610 |   }

===== M0175 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 670-723
Documentation:

Method code:
670 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
671 |     String varName = n.getString();
672 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
673 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
674 |     if (value != null) {
675 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
676 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
677 |           getJSType(value));
678 |       return scope;
679 |     } else {
680 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
681 |       if (var != null) {
682 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
683 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
684 | 
685 |         // 1) The var is escaped and assigned in an inner scope, e.g.,
686 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
687 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
688 |         boolean unflowable = isInferred &&
689 |             isUnflowable(syntacticScope.getVar(varName));
690 | 
691 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
692 |         // inferred variable. That variable is assigned more than once,
693 |         // and we can't know which type we're getting.
694 |         //
695 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); } doStuff(); t = {};
696 |         //
697 |         // Notice that this heuristic isn't perfect. For example, you might
698 |         // have:
699 |         //
700 |         // function f() { (t); } f(); var t = 3;
701 |         //
702 |         // In this case, we would infer the first reference to t as
703 |         // type {number}, even though it's undefined.
704 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot = false;
705 |         if (isInferred && syntacticScope.isLocal()) {
706 |           Var maybeOuterVar = syntacticScope.getParent().getVar(varName);
707 |           if (var == maybeOuterVar &&
708 |               !maybeOuterVar.isMarkedAssignedExactlyOnce()) {
709 |             nonLocalInferredSlot = true;
710 |           }
711 |         }
712 | 
713 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
714 |           type = var.getType();
715 |           if (type == null) {
716 |             type = unknownType;
717 |           }
718 |         }
719 |       }
720 |     }
721 |     n.setJSType(type);
722 |     return scope;
723 |   }

===== M0177 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 532-575
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
532 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
533 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
534 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
535 |     switch (left.getType()) {
536 |       case Token.NAME:
537 |         String varName = left.getString();
538 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
539 | 
540 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
541 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
542 |         // This has two purposes:
543 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
544 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
545 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
546 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
547 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
548 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
549 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
550 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
551 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
552 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
553 |         }
554 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
555 |             resultType : null);
556 | 
557 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
558 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
559 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
560 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
561 |         }
562 |         break;
563 |       case Token.GETPROP:
564 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
565 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
566 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
567 |               leftType == null ? unknownType : leftType,
568 |               resultType);
569 |         }
570 | 
571 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
572 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
573 |         break;
574 |     }
575 |   }

===== M0178 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 481-510
Documentation:

Method code:
481 |     @Override
482 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
483 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
484 |       if (n.isFunction() ||
485 |           n.isScript()) {
486 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
487 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
488 |       }
489 | 
490 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
491 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
492 |       boolean descend = parent == null || !parent.isFunction() ||
493 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
494 | 
495 |       if (descend) {
496 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
497 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
498 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
499 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
500 |                child != null;
501 |                child = child.getNext()) {
502 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
503 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child);
504 |             }
505 |           }
506 |         }
507 |       }
508 | 
509 |       return descend;
510 |     }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.

===== M0194 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1944-1972
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1944 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1945 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1946 |     Preconditions.checkState(!n.isFunction() ||
1947 |             type.isFunctionType() ||
1948 |             type.isUnknownType());
1949 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1950 |     if (info != null) {
1951 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1952 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Change this so that we only look for casts on CAST
1953 |         // nodes one the misplaced type annotation warning is on by default and
1954 |         // people have been given a chance to fix them.  As is, this is here
1955 |         // simply for legacy casts.
1956 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1957 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1958 |         type = infoType;
1959 |       }
1960 | 
1961 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1962 |         String propName = n.isGetProp() ?
1963 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1964 |         compiler.report(
1965 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1966 |       }
1967 |     }
1968 | 
1969 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1970 |       n.setJSType(type);
1971 |     }
1972 |   }

===== M0196 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1718-1752
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1718 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1719 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1720 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1721 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1722 | 
1723 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1724 |     int ordinal = 0;
1725 |     Node parameter = null;
1726 |     Node argument = null;
1727 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1728 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1729 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1730 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1731 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1732 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1733 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1734 |       }
1735 |       argument = arguments.next();
1736 |       ordinal++;
1737 | 
1738 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1739 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1740 |     }
1741 | 
1742 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1743 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1744 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1745 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1746 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1747 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1748 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1749 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1750 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1751 |     }
1752 |   }

===== M0232 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isStruct()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 297-316
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true iff {@code this} can be a {@code struct}.
   * UnionType overrides the method, assume {@code this} is not a union here.
   */
Method code:
297 |   public boolean isStruct() {
298 |     if (isObject()) {
299 |       ObjectType objType = toObjectType();
300 |       ObjectType iproto = objType.getImplicitPrototype();
301 |       // For the case when a @struct constructor is assigned to a function's
302 |       // prototype property
303 |       if (iproto != null && iproto.isStruct()) {
304 |         return true;
305 |       }
306 |       FunctionType ctor = objType.getConstructor();
307 |       // This test is true for object literals
308 |       if (ctor == null) {
309 |         JSDocInfo info = objType.getJSDocInfo();
310 |         return info != null && info.makesStructs();
311 |       } else {
312 |         return ctor.makesStructs();
313 |       }
314 |     }
315 |     return false;
316 |   }
