===== M0271 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.finishConstructorDefinition(Node,String,FunctionType,Scope,CompilerInput,Var)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1206-1250
Documentation:

Method code:
1206 |     private void finishConstructorDefinition(
1207 |         Node n, String variableName, FunctionType fnType,
1208 |         Scope scopeToDeclareIn, CompilerInput input, Var newVar) {
1209 |       // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1210 |       FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1211 |       Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1212 | 
1213 |       // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1214 |       // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1215 |       // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1216 |       // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1217 |       // the same node.
1218 |       //
1219 |       // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1220 |       // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1221 |       prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1222 | 
1223 |       String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1224 | 
1225 |       // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1226 |       // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1227 |       // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1228 |       Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1229 |       if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1230 |         scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1231 |       }
1232 | 
1233 |       scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1234 |           n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1235 |           /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1236 |           superClassCtor == null ||
1237 |           superClassCtor.getInstanceType().isEquivalentTo(
1238 |               getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1239 | 
1240 |       // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1241 |       // it constructs itself.
1242 |       if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1243 |           !n.isFromExterns()) {
1244 |         compiler.report(
1245 |             JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1246 |                 fnType.isConstructor() ?
1247 |                 CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1248 |                 variableName));
1249 |       }
1250 |     }

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1283-1337
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1283 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1284 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1285 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1286 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1287 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1288 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1289 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1290 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1291 |       } else if (info != null) {
1292 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1293 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1294 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1295 |           } else {
1296 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1297 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1298 |           }
1299 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1300 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1301 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1302 |         } else {
1303 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1304 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1305 |             JSType knownType = null;
1306 |             if (rValue != null) {
1307 |               JSDocInfo rValueInfo = rValue.getJSDocInfo();
1308 |               if (rValueInfo != null && rValueInfo.hasType()) {
1309 |                 // If rValue has a type-cast, we use the type in the type-cast.
1310 |                 return rValueInfo.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
1311 |               } else if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1312 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1313 |                 // If rValue's type was already computed during scope creation,
1314 |                 // then we can safely use that.
1315 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1316 |               } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1317 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1318 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1319 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1320 |                 // reasons.
1321 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1322 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1323 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1324 |                     && lValue.isName()
1325 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1326 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1327 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1328 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1329 |                 }
1330 |               }
1331 |             }
1332 |           }
1333 |         }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 565-585
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
565 |   @Override
566 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
567 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
568 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
569 |     if (unknown) {
570 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
571 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
572 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
573 |         unknown = false;
574 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
575 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
576 |             unknown = true;
577 |             break;
578 |           }
579 |         }
580 |       } else {
581 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
582 |       }
583 |     }
584 |     return unknown;
585 |   }

===== M0295 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.describeFunctionBind(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 385-424
Documentation:

Method code:
385 |   @Override
386 |   public Bind describeFunctionBind(Node n, boolean useTypeInfo) {
387 |     Bind result = super.describeFunctionBind(n, useTypeInfo);
388 |     if (result != null) {
389 |       return result;
390 |     }
391 | 
392 |     if (!n.isCall()) {
393 |       return null;
394 |     }
395 | 
396 |     Node callTarget = n.getFirstChild();
397 |     String name = callTarget.getQualifiedName();
398 |     if (name != null) {
399 |       if (name.equals("goog.bind")
400 |           || name.equals("goog$bind")) {
401 |         // goog.bind(fn, self, args...);
402 |         Node fn = callTarget.getNext();
403 |         if (fn == null) {
404 |           return null;
405 |         }
406 |         Node thisValue = safeNext(fn);
407 |         Node parameters = safeNext(thisValue);
408 |         return new Bind(fn, thisValue, parameters);
409 |       }
410 | 
411 |       if (name.equals("goog.partial") || name.equals("goog$partial")) {
412 |         // goog.partial(fn, args...);
413 |         Node fn = callTarget.getNext();
414 |         if (fn == null) {
415 |           return null;
416 |         }
417 |         Node thisValue = null;
418 |         Node parameters = safeNext(fn);
419 |         return new Bind(fn, thisValue, parameters);
420 |       }
421 |     }
422 | 
423 |     return null;
424 |   }

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 470-846
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
470 |   @Override
471 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
472 |     JSType childType;
473 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
474 |     Node left, right;
475 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
476 |     boolean typeable = true;
477 | 
478 |     switch (n.getType()) {
479 |       case Token.CAST:
480 |         Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
481 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(expr));
482 | 
483 |         // If the cast, tightens the type apply it, so it is available post
484 |         // normalization.
485 |         JSType castType = getJSType(n);
486 |         JSType exprType = getJSType(expr);
487 |         if (castType.isSubtype(exprType)) {
488 |           expr.setJSType(castType);
489 |         }
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.NAME:
493 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
494 |         break;
495 | 
496 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
497 |         typeable = false;
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.COMMA:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.TRUE:
505 |       case Token.FALSE:
506 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
507 |         break;
508 | 
509 |       case Token.THIS:
510 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
511 |         break;
512 | 
513 |       case Token.NULL:
514 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
515 |         break;
516 | 
517 |       case Token.NUMBER:
518 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
519 |         break;
520 | 
521 |       case Token.STRING:
522 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
523 |         break;
524 | 
525 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
526 |         typeable = false;
527 |         break;
528 | 
529 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
530 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
531 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
532 |         break;
533 | 
534 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
535 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
536 |         break;
537 | 
538 |       case Token.REGEXP:
539 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
540 |         break;
541 | 
542 |       case Token.GETPROP:
543 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
544 |         typeable = !(parent.isAssign() &&
545 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
546 |         break;
547 | 
548 |       case Token.GETELEM:
549 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
550 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
551 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
552 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
553 |         typeable = false;
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.VAR:
557 |         visitVar(t, n);
558 |         typeable = false;
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.NEW:
562 |         visitNew(t, n);
563 |         break;
564 | 
565 |       case Token.CALL:
566 |         visitCall(t, n);
567 |         typeable = !parent.isExprResult();
568 |         break;
569 | 
570 |       case Token.RETURN:
571 |         visitReturn(t, n);
572 |         typeable = false;
573 |         break;
574 | 
575 |       case Token.DEC:
576 |       case Token.INC:
577 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
578 |         checkPropCreation(t, left);
579 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
580 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
581 |         break;
582 | 
583 |       case Token.NOT:
584 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
585 |         break;
586 | 
587 |       case Token.VOID:
588 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
589 |         break;
590 | 
591 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
592 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
593 |         break;
594 | 
595 |       case Token.BITNOT:
596 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
597 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
598 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
599 |               childType.toString());
600 |         }
601 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
602 |         break;
603 | 
604 |       case Token.POS:
605 |       case Token.NEG:
606 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
607 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
608 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
609 |         break;
610 | 
611 |       case Token.EQ:
612 |       case Token.NE:
613 |       case Token.SHEQ:
614 |       case Token.SHNE: {
615 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
616 |         right = n.getLastChild();
617 | 
618 |         if (left.isTypeOf()) {
619 |           if (right.isString()) {
620 |             checkTypeofString(t, right, right.getString());
621 |           }
622 |         } else if (right.isTypeOf() && left.isString()) {
623 |           checkTypeofString(t, left, left.getString());
624 |         }
625 | 
626 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
627 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
628 | 
629 |         // We do not want to warn about explicit comparisons to VOID. People
630 |         // often do this if they think their type annotations screwed up.
631 |         //
632 |         // We do want to warn about cases where people compare things like
633 |         // (Array|null) == (Function|null)
634 |         // because it probably means they screwed up.
635 |         //
636 |         // This heuristic here is not perfect, but should catch cases we
637 |         // care about without too many false negatives.
638 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
639 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
640 | 
641 |         TernaryValue result = TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
642 |         if (n.getType() == Token.EQ || n.getType() == Token.NE) {
643 |           result = leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
644 |           if (n.isNE()) {
645 |             result = result.not();
646 |           }
647 |         } else {
648 |           // SHEQ or SHNE
649 |           if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
650 |                   rightTypeRestricted)) {
651 |             result = n.getType() == Token.SHEQ ?
652 |                 TernaryValue.FALSE : TernaryValue.TRUE;
653 |           }
654 |         }
655 | 
656 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
657 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
658 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
659 |         }
660 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
661 |         break;
662 |       }
663 | 
664 |       case Token.LT:
665 |       case Token.LE:
666 |       case Token.GT:
667 |       case Token.GE:
668 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
669 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
670 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
671 |           validator.expectNumber(
672 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
673 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
674 |           validator.expectNumber(
675 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
676 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
677 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
678 |           // OK.
679 |         } else {
680 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
681 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
682 |           // should match a string context.
683 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
684 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
685 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
686 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
687 |           message = "right side of comparison";
688 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
689 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
690 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
691 |         }
692 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
693 |         break;
694 | 
695 |       case Token.IN:
696 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
697 |         right = n.getLastChild();
698 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
699 |         validator.expectString(t, left, getJSType(left), "left side of 'in'");
700 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
701 |         if (rightType.isStruct()) {
702 |           report(t, right, IN_USED_WITH_STRUCT);
703 |         }
704 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
705 |         break;
706 | 
707 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
708 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
709 |         right = n.getLastChild();
710 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
711 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
712 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "deterministic instanceof yields false");
713 |         validator.expectActualObject(
714 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
715 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
716 |         break;
717 | 
718 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
719 |         visitAssign(t, n);
720 |         typeable = false;
721 |         break;
722 | 
723 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
724 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
725 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
726 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
727 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
728 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
729 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
730 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
731 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
732 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
733 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
734 |         checkPropCreation(t, n.getFirstChild());
735 |         // fall through
736 | 
737 |       case Token.LSH:
738 |       case Token.RSH:
739 |       case Token.URSH:
740 |       case Token.DIV:
741 |       case Token.MOD:
742 |       case Token.BITOR:
743 |       case Token.BITXOR:
744 |       case Token.BITAND:
745 |       case Token.SUB:
746 |       case Token.ADD:
747 |       case Token.MUL:
748 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
749 |         break;
750 | 
751 |       case Token.DELPROP:
752 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
753 |         break;
754 | 
755 |       case Token.CASE:
756 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
757 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
758 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
759 |         typeable = false;
760 |         break;
761 | 
762 |       case Token.WITH: {
763 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
764 |         childType = getJSType(child);
765 |         validator.expectObject(t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
766 |         typeable = false;
767 |         break;
768 |       }
769 | 
770 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
771 |         visitFunction(t, n);
772 |         break;
773 | 
774 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
775 |       case Token.LABEL:
776 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
777 |       case Token.SWITCH:
778 |       case Token.BREAK:
779 |       case Token.CATCH:
780 |       case Token.TRY:
781 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
782 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
783 |       case Token.BLOCK:
784 |       case Token.EMPTY:
785 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
786 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
787 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
788 |       case Token.THROW:
789 |         typeable = false;
790 |         break;
791 | 
792 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
793 |       case Token.DO:
794 |       case Token.IF:
795 |       case Token.WHILE:
796 |         typeable = false;
797 |         break;
798 | 
799 |       case Token.FOR:
800 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
801 |           Node obj = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
802 |           if (getJSType(obj).isStruct()) {
803 |             report(t, obj, IN_USED_WITH_STRUCT);
804 |           }
805 |         }
806 |         typeable = false;
807 |         break;
808 | 
809 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
810 |       case Token.AND:
811 |       case Token.HOOK:
812 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
813 |       case Token.OR:
814 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
815 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
816 |         } else {
817 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
818 |           if ((n.isObjectLit())
819 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
820 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
821 |           } else {
822 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
823 |           }
824 |         }
825 |         if (n.isObjectLit()) {
826 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
827 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
828 |           }
829 |         }
830 |         break;
831 | 
832 |       default:
833 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
834 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
835 |         break;
836 |     }
837 | 
838 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
839 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
840 | 
841 |     if (typeable) {
842 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
843 |     }
844 | 
845 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
846 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
785 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
786 |     if (item == null) {
787 |       return null;
788 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
789 |       return item.getNext();
790 |     } else {
791 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
792 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
793 |         return item.chain(result);
794 |       } else {
795 |         return item;
796 |       }
797 |     }
798 |   }

===== M0322 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0324 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 396-421
Documentation:

Method code:
396 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
397 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
398 |     // two cases:
399 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
400 |     // and
401 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
402 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
403 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
404 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
405 |     //
406 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
407 |     //
408 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
409 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
410 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
411 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
412 |     //
413 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
414 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
415 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
416 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
417 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
418 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
419 |     }
420 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
421 |   }

===== M0346 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 443-455
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;
455 |     }

===== M0348 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2383-2399
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2383 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2384 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2385 |     int c;
2386 |     while (true) {
2387 |       c = stream.getChar();
2388 |       if (c == ' ') {
2389 |         continue;
2390 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2391 |         matchedLc = true;
2392 |         break;
2393 |       } else {
2394 |         break;
2395 |       }
2396 |     }
2397 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2398 |     return matchedLc;
2399 |   }

===== M0358 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1660-1713
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1660 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1661 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1662 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1663 | 
1664 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1665 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1666 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1667 |       return;
1668 |     }
1669 | 
1670 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1671 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1672 |     if (childType.isFunctionType()) {
1673 |       FunctionType functionType = childType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1674 | 
1675 |       boolean isExtern = false;
1676 |       JSDocInfo functionJSDocInfo = functionType.getJSDocInfo();
1677 |       if( functionJSDocInfo != null  &&
1678 |           functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode() != null) {
1679 |         isExtern = functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode().isFromExterns();
1680 |       }
1681 | 
1682 |       // Non-native constructors should not be called directly
1683 |       // unless they specify a return type and are defined
1684 |       // in an extern.
1685 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1686 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType() &&
1687 |           (functionType.getReturnType().isUnknownType() ||
1688 |            functionType.getReturnType().isVoidType() ||
1689 |            !isExtern)) {
1690 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1691 |       }
1692 | 
1693 |       // Functions with explicit 'this' types must be called in a GETPROP
1694 |       // or GETELEM.
1695 |       if (functionType.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
1696 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType() &&
1697 |           !(functionType.getTypeOfThis().toObjectType() != null &&
1698 |           functionType.getTypeOfThis().toObjectType().isNativeObjectType()) &&
1699 |           !(child.isGetElem() ||
1700 |             child.isGetProp())) {
1701 |         report(t, n, EXPECTED_THIS_TYPE, functionType.toString());
1702 |       }
1703 | 
1704 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1705 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1706 |     } else {
1707 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1708 |     }
1709 | 
1710 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1711 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1712 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1713 |   }

325 |   public ObjectLiteralCast getObjectLiteralCast(Node callNode) {
326 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(callNode.isCall());
327 |     ObjectLiteralCast proxyCast = super.getObjectLiteralCast(callNode);
328 |     if (proxyCast != null) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 84-116
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
85 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
86 |                 Scope functionScope,
87 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap) {
88 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
89 |     this.compiler = compiler;
90 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
91 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
92 |     this.unknownType = registry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);

95 |     inferArguments(functionScope);
96 | 
97 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
98 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
99 | 
100 |     // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
101 |     // type is VOID.
102 |     Iterator<Var> varIt =
103 |         functionScope.getDeclarativelyUnboundVarsWithoutTypes();
104 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
105 |       Var var = varIt.next();
106 |       if (isUnflowable(var)) {
107 |         continue;
108 |       }
109 |

111 |           var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
112 |     }
113 | 
114 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
115 |         Scope.createLatticeBottom(functionScope.getRootNode()));
116 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.checkForClassDefiningCalls(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1359-1431
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1359 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1360 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1361 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1362 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1363 |       if (relationship != null) {
1364 |         FunctionType superCtor = getFunctionType(
1365 |             scope.getVar(relationship.superclassName));
1366 |         FunctionType subCtor = getFunctionType(
1367 |             scope.getVar(relationship.subclassName));
1368 |         if (superCtor != null && superCtor.isConstructor() &&
1369 |             subCtor != null && subCtor.isConstructor()) {
1370 |           ObjectType superClass = superCtor.getInstanceType();
1371 |           ObjectType subClass = subCtor.getInstanceType();
1372 | 
1373 |           // superCtor and subCtor might be structural constructors
1374 |           // (like {function(new:Object)}) so we need to resolve them back
1375 |           // to the original ctor objects.
1376 |           superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1377 |           subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1378 | 
1379 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS &&
1380 |               !superClass.isEmptyType() && !subClass.isEmptyType()) {
1381 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1382 |           }
1383 | 
1384 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1385 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1386 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1387 |           }
1388 |         }
1389 |       }
1390 | 
1391 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1392 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1393 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1394 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1395 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1396 |         if (objectType != null) {
1397 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1398 | 
1399 |           if (functionType != null) {
1400 |             FunctionType getterType =
1401 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1402 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1403 |                 objectType);
1404 |           }
1405 |         }
1406 |       }
1407 | 
1408 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1409 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1410 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1411 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1412 |       }
1413 | 
1414 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1415 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(n);
1416 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1417 |         if (objectLiteralCast.diagnosticType == null) {
1418 |           ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1419 |               typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1420 |           if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1421 |             setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1422 |           } else {
1423 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1424 |                     CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
1425 |           }
1426 |         } else {
1427 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1428 |                   objectLiteralCast.diagnosticType));
1429 |         }
1430 |       }
1431 |     }

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 309-370
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
309 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
310 |     if (info != null) {
311 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
312 |       makesStructs = info.makesStructs();
313 |       makesDicts = info.makesDicts();
314 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
315 | 
316 |       if (makesStructs && !isConstructor) {
317 |         reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@struct", fnName);
318 |       } else if (makesDicts && !isConstructor) {
319 |         reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@dict", fnName);
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       // base type
323 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
324 |         if (isConstructor) {
325 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
326 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
327 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
328 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
329 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
330 |           }
331 |         } else {
332 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
333 |         }
334 |       }
335 | 
336 |       // Implemented interfaces (for constructors only).
337 |       if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
338 |         if (isConstructor) {
339 |           implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
340 |           for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
341 |             JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
342 |             if (maybeInterType != null &&
343 |                 maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
344 |               implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
345 |             }
346 |           }
347 |         } else if (isInterface) {
348 |           reportWarning(
349 |               TypeCheck.CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, fnName);
350 |         } else {
351 |           reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@implements", fnName);
352 |         }
353 |       }
354 | 
355 |       // extended interfaces (for interfaces only)
356 |       // We've already emitted a warning if this is not an interface.
357 |       if (isInterface) {
358 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
359 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
360 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
361 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
362 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
363 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
364 |           }
365 |         }
366 |       }
367 |     }
368 | 
369 |     return this;
370 |   }

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkEnumAlias(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1872-1889
Documentation:
/**
   * <p>Checks enum aliases.
   *
   * <p>We verify that the enum element type of the enum used
   * for initialization is a subtype of the enum element type of
   * the enum the value is being copied in.</p>
   *
   * <p>Example:</p>
   * <pre>var myEnum = myOtherEnum;</pre>
   *
   * <p>Enum aliases are irregular, so we need special code for this :(</p>
   *
   * @param value the value used for initialization of the enum
   */
Method code:
1872 |   private void checkEnumAlias(
1873 |       NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo declInfo, Node value) {
1874 |     if (declInfo == null || !declInfo.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1875 |       return;
1876 |     }
1877 | 
1878 |     JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1879 |     if (!valueType.isEnumType()) {
1880 |       return;
1881 |     }
1882 | 
1883 |     EnumType valueEnumType = valueType.toMaybeEnumType();
1884 |     JSType valueEnumPrimitiveType =
1885 |         valueEnumType.getElementsType().getPrimitiveType();
1886 |     validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, value, valueEnumPrimitiveType,
1887 |         declInfo.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry),

===== M0408 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

===== M0425 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.isQualifiedNameInferred(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1620-1663
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a qualified name is inferred.
     * NOTE(nicksantos): Determining whether a property is declared or not
     * is really really obnoxious.
     *
     * The problem is that there are two (equally valid) coding styles:
     *
     * (function() {
     *   /* The authoritative definition of goog.bar. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * })();
     *
     * function f() {
     *   goog.bar();
     *   /* Reset goog.bar to a no-op. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * }
     *
     * In a dynamic language with first-class functions, it's very difficult
     * to know which one the user intended without looking at lots of
     * contextual information (the second example demonstrates a small case
     * of this, but there are some really pathological cases as well).
     *
     * The current algorithm checks if either the declaration has
     * JsDoc type information, or @const with a known type,
     * or a function literal with a name we haven't seen before.
     */
Method code:
1620 |     private boolean isQualifiedNameInferred(
1621 |         String qName, Node n, JSDocInfo info,
1622 |         Node rhsValue, JSType valueType) {
1623 |       if (valueType == null) {
1624 |         return true;
1625 |       }
1626 | 
1627 |       boolean inferred = true;
1628 |       if (info != null) {
1629 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1630 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1631 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1632 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1633 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1634 |       }
1635 | 
1636 |       if (inferred && rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isFunction()) {
1637 |         if (info != null) {
1638 |           return false;
1639 |         } else if (!scope.isDeclared(qName, false) &&
1640 |             n.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
1641 | 
1642 |           // Check if this is in a conditional block.
1643 |           // Functions assigned in conditional blocks are inferred.
1644 |           for (Node current = n.getParent();
1645 |                !(current.isScript() || current.isFunction());
1646 |                current = current.getParent()) {
1647 |             if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
1648 |               return true;
1649 |             }
1650 |           }
1651 | 
1652 |           // Check if this is assigned in an inner scope.
1653 |           // Functions assigned in inner scopes are inferred.
1654 |           AstFunctionContents contents =
1655 |               getFunctionAnalysisResults(scope.getRootNode());
1656 |           if (contents == null ||
1657 |               !contents.getEscapedQualifiedNames().contains(qName)) {
1658 |             return false;
1659 |           }
1660 |         }
1661 |       }
1662 |       return inferred;
1663 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.report(JSError)

2069 |   public void report(JSError error) {

2072 |       CheckLevel newLevel = warningsGuard.level(error);
2073 |       if (newLevel != null) {
2074 |         level = newLevel;
2075 |       }
2076 |     }
2077 |

2079 |       if (getOptions().errorHandler != null) {
2080 |         getOptions().errorHandler.report(level, error);
2081 |       }
2082 |       errorManager.report(level, error);
2083 |     }
2084 |   }

===== M0428 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 665-706
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
665 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
666 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
667 |         fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), parametersNode, returnType, null);
668 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
669 | 
670 |     if (makesStructs) {
671 |       fnType.setStruct();
672 |     } else if (makesDicts) {
673 |       fnType.setDict();
674 |     }
675 |     if (existingType != null) {
676 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType.isInstanceType();
677 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
678 |         FunctionType existingFn =
679 |             isInstanceObject ?
680 |             existingType.toObjectType().getConstructor() :
681 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
682 | 
683 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
684 |           existingFn.setSource(contents.getSourceNode());
685 |         }
686 | 
687 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
688 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
689 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
690 |         }
691 | 
692 |         return existingFn;
693 |       } else {
694 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
695 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
696 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
697 |       }
698 |     }
699 | 
700 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
701 | 
702 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
703 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
704 |     }
705 |     return fnType;
706 |   }

   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left

===== M0458 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInferencePass.inferAllScopes(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInferencePass.java
Original range: 77-107
Documentation:
/** Entry point for type inference when running over part of the tree. */
Method code:
77 |   void inferAllScopes(Node node) {
78 |     // Type analysis happens in two major phases.
79 |     // 1) Finding all the symbols.
80 |     // 2) Propagating all the inferred types.
81 |     //
82 |     // The order of this analysis is non-obvious. In a complete inference
83 |     // system, we may need to backtrack arbitrarily far. But the compile-time
84 |     // costs would be unacceptable.
85 |     //
86 |     // We do one pass where we do typed scope creation for all scopes
87 |     // in pre-order.
88 |     //
89 |     // Then we do a second pass where we do all type inference
90 |     // (type propagation) in pre-order.
91 |     //
92 |     // We use a memoized scope creator so that we never create a scope
93 |     // more than once.
94 |     //
95 |     // This will allow us to handle cases like:
96 |     // var ns = {};
97 |     // (function() { /** JSDoc */ ns.method = function() {}; })();
98 |     // ns.method();
99 |     // In this code, we need to build the symbol table for the inner scope in
100 |     // order to propagate the type of ns.method in the outer scope.
101 |     (new NodeTraversal(
102 |         compiler, new FirstScopeBuildingCallback(), scopeCreator))
103 |         .traverseWithScope(node, topScope);
104 |     (new NodeTraversal(
105 |         compiler, new SecondScopeBuildingCallback(), scopeCreator))
106 |         .traverseWithScope(node, topScope);
107 |   }

===== M0461 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1769-1795
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1769 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1770 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1771 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1772 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1773 |       skipEOLs();
1774 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1775 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1776 |       skipEOLs();
1777 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1778 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1779 |       skipEOLs();
1780 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1781 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1782 |       String string = stream.getString();
1783 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1784 |         skipEOLs();
1785 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1786 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1787 |         return newStringNode(string);
1788 |       } else {
1789 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1790 |       }
1791 |     }
1792 | 
1793 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1794 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1795 |   }

===== M0464 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1328-1357
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1328 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1329 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1330 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1331 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1332 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1333 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1334 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1335 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1336 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1337 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1338 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1339 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1340 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1341 |       return false;
1342 |     }
1343 | 
1344 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1345 |     if (type == null) {
1346 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1347 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1348 |       if (var != null) {
1349 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1350 |         if (varType != null) {
1351 |           type = varType;
1352 |         }
1353 |       }
1354 |     }
1355 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1356 |     return true;
1357 |   }

===== M0465 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1116-1143
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infima, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
1116 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
1117 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
1118 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
1119 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
1120 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
1121 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
1122 |       UnionType unionType = type.toMaybeUnionType();
1123 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
1124 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
1125 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
1126 |           needsFiltering = true;
1127 |           break;
1128 |         }
1129 |       }
1130 | 
1131 |       if (needsFiltering) {
1132 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
1133 |         builder.addAlternate(type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE));
1134 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
1135 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
1136 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
1137 |           }
1138 |         }
1139 |         return builder.build();
1140 |       }
1141 |     }
1142 |     return type;
1143 |   }

===== M0467 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 853-961
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the JsDoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
853 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
854 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
855 |         @Nullable String name,
856 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
857 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
858 | 
859 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
860 | 
861 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
862 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
863 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
864 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
865 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
866 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
867 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
868 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
869 |             functionType = aliasedType;
870 | 
871 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
872 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
873 |             }
874 |           }
875 |         }
876 |       }
877 | 
878 |       if (functionType == null) {
879 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
880 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
881 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
882 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
883 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
884 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
885 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
886 | 
887 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
888 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
889 | 
890 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
891 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
892 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
893 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
894 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
895 |           }
896 |         }
897 | 
898 |         if (functionType == null) {
899 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
900 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
901 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
902 |           Var ownerVar = null;
903 |           String propName = null;
904 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
905 |           if (ownerName != null) {
906 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
907 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
908 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
909 |             }
910 |             if (name != null) {
911 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
912 |             }
913 |           }
914 | 
915 |           FunctionType overriddenType = null;
916 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
917 |             overriddenType = findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
918 |           }
919 | 
920 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
921 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
922 |                   scope)
923 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
924 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenType, parametersNode)
925 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
926 |               .inferReturnType(info)
927 |               .inferInheritance(info);
928 | 
929 |           // Infer the context type.
930 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
931 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType() &&
932 |               ownerType.getOwnerFunction().hasInstanceType()) {
933 |             builder.inferThisType(
934 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
935 |             searchedForThisType = true;
936 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
937 |             // If 'this' has a type, use that instead.
938 |             // This is a hack, necessary because CollectProperties (below)
939 |             // doesn't run with the scope that it's building,
940 |             // so scope.getTypeOfThis() will be wrong.
941 |             JSType injectedThisType = ownerNode.getJSType();
942 |             builder.inferThisType(
943 |                 info,
944 |                 injectedThisType == null ?
945 |                 scope.getTypeOfThis() : injectedThisType);
946 |             searchedForThisType = true;
947 |           }
948 | 
949 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
950 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
951 |           }
952 | 
953 |           functionType = builder
954 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
955 |               .buildAndRegister();
956 |         }
957 |       }
958 | 
959 |       // all done
960 |       return functionType;
961 |     }

   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.isAssign()</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
883 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
884 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
885 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
886 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
887 | 
888 |     // Check property sets to 'object.property' when 'object' is known.
889 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
890 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
891 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
892 |       Node property = lvalue.getLastChild();
893 |       String pname = property.getString();
894 | 
895 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
896 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
897 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
898 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
899 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
900 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
901 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, pname, lvalue, rvalue);
902 |         }
903 |       }
904 | 
905 |       checkEnumAlias(t, info, rvalue);
906 |       checkPropCreation(t, lvalue);
907 | 
908 |       // Prototype assignments are special, because they actually affect
909 |       // the definition of a class. These are mostly validated
910 |       // during TypedScopeCreator, and we only look for the "dumb" cases here.
911 |       // object.prototype = ...;
912 |       if (pname.equals("prototype")) {
913 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
914 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
915 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
916 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
917 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
918 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
919 |             // Only assign structs to the prototype of a @struct constructor
920 |             if (functionType.makesStructs() && !rvalueType.isStruct()) {
921 |               String funName = functionType.getTypeOfThis().toString();
922 |               compiler.report(t.makeError(assign, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
923 |                                           "struct", funName));
924 |             }
925 |             return;
926 |           }
927 |         }
928 |       }
929 | 
930 |       // The generic checks for 'object.property' when 'object' is known,
931 |       // and 'property' is declared on it.
932 |       // object.property = ...;
933 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
934 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
935 |       if (type != null) {
936 |         if (type.hasProperty(pname) &&
937 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(pname) &&
938 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, pname)) {
939 |           JSType expectedType = type.getPropertyType(pname);
940 |           if (!expectedType.isUnknownType()) {
941 |             validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
942 |                 t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
943 |                 expectedType, object, pname);
944 |             checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
945 |                 t, assign, object, pname, info, expectedType);
946 |             return;
947 |           }
948 |         }
949 |       }
950 | 
951 |       // If we couldn't get the property type with normal object property
952 |       // lookups, then check inheritance anyway with the unknown type.
953 |       checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
954 |           t, assign, object, pname, info, getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
955 |     }
956 | 
957 |     // Check qualified name sets to 'object' and 'object.property'.
958 |     // This can sometimes handle cases when the type of 'object' is not known.
959 |     // e.g.,
960 |     // var obj = createUnknownType();
961 |     // /** @type {number} */ obj.foo = true;
962 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
963 |     if (lvalue.isQualifiedName()) {
964 |       // variable with inferred type case
965 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
966 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getQualifiedName());
967 |       if (var != null) {
968 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
969 |           return;
970 |         }
971 | 
972 |         if (NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(lvalue).isThis() &&
973 |             t.getScope() != var.getScope()) {
974 |           // Don't look at "this.foo" variables from other scopes.
975 |           return;
976 |         }
977 | 
978 |         if (var.getType() != null) {
979 |           leftType = var.getType();
980 |         }
981 |       }
982 |     }
983 | 
984 |     // Fall through case for arbitrary LHS and arbitrary RHS.
985 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
986 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
987 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
988 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
989 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
990 |     } else {
991 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
992 |     }
993 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.ProvidedName.replace()

Original range: 855-918
Documentation:
/**
     * Replace the provide statement.
     *
     * If we're providing a name with no definition, then create one.
     * If we're providing a name with a duplicate definition, then make sure
     * that definition becomes a declaration.
     */
Method code:
855 |     void replace() {
856 |       if (firstNode == null) {
857 |         // Don't touch the base case ('goog').
858 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
859 |         return;
860 |       }
861 | 
862 |       // Handle the case where there is a duplicate definition for an explicitly
863 |       // provided symbol.
864 |       if (candidateDefinition != null && explicitNode != null) {
865 |         explicitNode.detachFromParent();
866 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
867 | 
868 |         // Does this need a VAR keyword?
869 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
870 |         if (candidateDefinition.isExprResult() &&
871 |             !candidateDefinition.getFirstChild().isQualifiedName()) {
872 |           candidateDefinition.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE, true);
873 |           Node assignNode = candidateDefinition.getFirstChild();
874 |           Node nameNode = assignNode.getFirstChild();
875 |           if (nameNode.isName()) {
876 |             // Need to convert this assign to a var declaration.
877 |             Node valueNode = nameNode.getNext();
878 |             assignNode.removeChild(nameNode);
879 |             assignNode.removeChild(valueNode);
880 |             nameNode.addChildToFront(valueNode);
881 |             Node varNode = IR.var(nameNode);
882 |             varNode.copyInformationFrom(candidateDefinition);
883 |             candidateDefinition.getParent().replaceChild(
884 |                 candidateDefinition, varNode);
885 |             nameNode.setJSDocInfo(assignNode.getJSDocInfo());
886 |             compiler.reportCodeChange();
887 |             replacementNode = varNode;
888 |           }
889 |         }
890 |       } else {
891 |         // Handle the case where there's not a duplicate definition.
892 |         replacementNode = createDeclarationNode();
893 |         if (firstModule == minimumModule) {
894 |           firstNode.getParent().addChildBefore(replacementNode, firstNode);
895 |         } else {
896 |           // In this case, the name was implicitly provided by two independent
897 |           // modules. We need to move this code up to a common module.
898 |           int indexOfDot = namespace.lastIndexOf('.');
899 |           if (indexOfDot == -1) {
900 |             // Any old place is fine.
901 |             compiler.getNodeForCodeInsertion(minimumModule)
902 |                 .addChildToBack(replacementNode);
903 |           } else {
904 |             // Add it after the parent namespace.
905 |             ProvidedName parentName =
906 |                 providedNames.get(namespace.substring(0, indexOfDot));
907 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName);
908 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName.replacementNode);
909 |             parentName.replacementNode.getParent().addChildAfter(
910 |                 replacementNode, parentName.replacementNode);
911 |           }
912 |         }
913 |         if (explicitNode != null) {
914 |           explicitNode.detachFromParent();
915 |         }
916 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
917 |       }
918 |     }

/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
547 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
548 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
549 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
550 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
551 |     if (isOptional) {
552 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
553 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
554 |       // error can be reported.
555 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
556 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
557 |         emittedWarning = true;
558 |       }
559 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
560 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
561 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
562 |         emittedWarning = true;
563 |       }
564 |     } else {
565 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
566 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
567 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
568 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
569 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
570 |         } else {
571 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
572 |         }
573 |         emittedWarning = true;
574 |       }
575 |     }
576 |     return emittedWarning;
577 |   }

===== M0503 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1305-1355
Documentation:

Method code:
1305 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1306 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1307 |     // We often have a couple of different types to choose from for the
1308 |     // property. Ordered by accuracy, we have
1309 |     // 1) A locally inferred qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1310 |     // 2) A globally declared qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1311 |     // 3) A property on the owner type (which is on objType)
1312 |     // 4) A name in the type registry (as a last resort)
1313 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1314 |     boolean isLocallyInferred = false;
1315 | 
1316 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1317 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1318 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1319 |     if (var != null) {
1320 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1321 |       if (varType != null) {
1322 |         boolean isDeclared = !var.isTypeInferred();
1323 |         isLocallyInferred = (var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName));
1324 |         if (isDeclared || isLocallyInferred) {
1325 |           propertyType = varType;
1326 |         }
1327 |       }
1328 |     }
1329 | 
1330 |     if (propertyType == null && objType != null) {
1331 |       JSType foundType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1332 |       if (foundType != null) {
1333 |         propertyType = foundType;
1334 |       }
1335 |     }
1336 | 
1337 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType())
1338 |         && qualifiedName != null) {
1339 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1340 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1341 |       if (regType != null) {
1342 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1343 |       }
1344 |     }
1345 | 
1346 |     if (propertyType == null) {
1347 |       return unknownType;
1348 |     } else if (propertyType.isEquivalentTo(unknownType) && isLocallyInferred) {
1349 |       // If the type has been checked in this scope,
1350 |       // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1351 |       return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1352 |     } else {
1353 |       return propertyType;
1354 |     }
1355 |   }
