===== M0512 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // parameterized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isParameterizedType() || current.isParameterizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isParameterizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isParameterizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isParameterizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isParameterizedType());
224 |                 ParameterizedType parameterizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeParameterizedType();
225 |                 ParameterizedType parameterizedCurrent = current.toMaybeParameterizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (parameterizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(parameterizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   JSType alternateTypeParameter = parameterizedAlternate.getParameterType();
229 |                   JSType currentTypeParameter = parameterizedCurrent.getParameterType();
230 |                   if (currentTypeParameter.isEquivalentTo(parameterizedCurrent)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the parameterized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = parameterizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both parameterized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1176-1184
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1176 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1177 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1178 |       return -1;
1179 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1180 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1181 |     } else {
1182 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1183 |     }
1184 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1106-1204
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1106 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1107 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1108 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1109 | 
1110 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1111 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1112 |           isGlobalVar &&
1113 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1114 |            parent.isFunction());
1115 | 
1116 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1117 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1118 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1119 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1120 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1121 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1122 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1123 | 
1124 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1125 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1126 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1127 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1128 |         }
1129 |       }
1130 | 
1131 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1132 |       // the extern info from the node.
1133 |       boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1134 |       Var newVar = null;
1135 | 
1136 |       // declared in closest scope?
1137 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1138 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1139 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1140 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1141 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1142 |       } else {
1143 |         if (type != null) {
1144 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1145 |         }
1146 | 
1147 |         newVar =
1148 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1149 | 
1150 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1151 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1152 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1153 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1154 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1155 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1156 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1157 |           }
1158 |         }
1159 |       }
1160 | 
1161 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1162 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1163 |       if (fnType != null &&
1164 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1165 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1166 | 
1167 |         // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance type
1168 |         // (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1169 |         // This doesn't apply to structural constructors (like
1170 |         // function(new:Array). Checking the constructed type against
1171 |         // the variable name is a sufficient check for this.
1172 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1173 |             variableName.equals(fnType.getReferenceName())) {
1174 |           finishConstructorDefinition(n, variableName, fnType, scopeToDeclareIn,
1175 |                                       input, newVar);
1176 |         }
1177 |       }
1178 | 
1179 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1180 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1181 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1182 |         if (inferred) {
1183 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1184 |               type == null ?
1185 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1186 |               type,
1187 |               n);
1188 |         } else {
1189 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1190 |         }
1191 |       }
1192 | 
1193 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1194 |           && type != null
1195 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1196 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1197 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1198 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1199 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1200 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1201 |         globalThisCtor
1202 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1203 |       }
1204 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }

94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();

96 |

98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);

110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body

114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);

/**

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParameters(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 999-1028
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
999 |   private void updateTypeOfParameters(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
1000 |     int i = 0;
1001 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
1002 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
1003 |       if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
1004 |         // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
1005 |         return;
1006 |       }
1007 | 
1008 |       JSType iParameterType = getJSType(iParameter);
1009 |       Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
1010 |       JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
1011 |       inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(iArgumentType, iParameterType);
1012 | 
1013 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Filter out non-function types
1014 |       // (such as null and undefined) as
1015 |       // we only care about FUNCTION subtypes here.
1016 |       JSType restrictedParameter = iParameterType
1017 |           .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined()
1018 |           .toMaybeFunctionType();
1019 |       if (restrictedParameter != null) {
1020 |         if (iArgument.isFunction() &&
1021 |             iArgumentType.isFunctionType() &&
1022 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
1023 |           iArgument.setJSType(restrictedParameter);
1024 |         }
1025 |       }
1026 |       i++;
1027 |     }
1028 |   }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0568 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 92-189
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   @Override
93 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
94 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
95 | 
96 |     switch (n.getType()) {
97 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
98 |       case Token.NAME:
99 |         if (parent == null) {
100 |           return;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
104 |         if (!parent.isVar() &&
105 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
106 |             !(parent.isAssign() &&
107 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
108 |           return;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
112 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
113 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
114 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
115 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
116 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
117 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
118 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
119 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
120 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
121 |         if (docInfo == null &&
122 |             !(parent.isVar() &&
123 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
124 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
128 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
129 |         if (varType == null && parent.isFunction()) {
130 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
134 |         // we can do.
135 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
140 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
141 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
142 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
147 |         break;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
150 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
151 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
152 |         //
153 |         // 1)
154 |         // /** @deprecated */
155 |         // obj.prop = ...
156 |         //
157 |         // 2)
158 |         // /** @deprecated */
159 |         // obj.prop;
160 |         if (parent.isExprResult() ||
161 |             (parent.isAssign() &&
162 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
163 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
164 |           if (docInfo == null) {
165 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
166 |           }
167 |           if (docInfo != null) {
168 |             ObjectType lhsType =
169 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
170 |             if (lhsType != null) {
171 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
172 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
173 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
174 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo);
175 |               }
176 | 
177 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
178 |               ObjectType propType =
179 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
180 |               if (propType != null) {
181 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
182 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
183 |               }
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |         break;
188 |     }
189 |   }

===== M0575 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 94-153
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
94 |   @Override
95 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
96 |     SubclassRelationship relationship =
97 |         super.getClassesDefinedByCall(callNode);
98 |     if (relationship != null) return relationship;
99 | 
100 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
101 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
102 |     if (type != null) {
103 |       Node subclass = null;
104 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
105 | 
106 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
107 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
108 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
109 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
110 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
111 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
112 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
113 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
114 |           callName.isGetProp();
115 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
116 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
117 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
118 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
119 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
120 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
121 |       } else {
122 |         return null;
123 |       }
124 | 
125 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
126 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
127 |         // inheritance.
128 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
129 |           return null;
130 |         }
131 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
132 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
133 |             return null;
134 |           }
135 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
136 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
137 |         }
138 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
139 |       }
140 | 
141 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
142 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
143 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
144 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
145 |       if (subclass != null &&
146 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
147 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
148 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
149 |       }
150 |     }
151 | 
152 |     return null;
153 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 884-920
Documentation:

Method code:
884 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
885 |       Node callNode) {
886 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
887 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
888 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
889 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
890 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
891 |       return scope;
892 |     }
893 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
894 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
895 |       return scope;
896 |     }
897 |     JSType assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType(
898 |         callNode, registry);
899 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
900 | 
901 |     JSType narrowed;
902 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
903 |     if (assertedType == null) {
904 |       // Handle arbitrary expressions within the assert.
905 |       scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
906 |           assertedNode, scope, true);
907 |       // Build the result of the assertExpression
908 |       narrowed = getJSType(assertedNode).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
909 |     } else {
910 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
911 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
912 |       narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(assertedType);
913 |       if (assertedNodeName != null && type.differsFrom(narrowed)) {
914 |         scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
915 |       }
916 |     }
917 | 
918 |     callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
919 |     return scope;
920 |   }

89 |       ComposeWarningsGuard composeGuard = (ComposeWarningsGuard) guard;
90 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {
91 |         this.demoteErrors = composeGuard.demoteErrors;
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
95 |       addGuards(Lists.newArrayList(composeGuard.guards.descendingSet()));
96 |     } else {
97 |       numberOfAdds++;
98 |       orderOfAddition.put(guard, numberOfAdds);
99 |       guards.remove(guard);
100 |       guards.add(guard);
101 |     }
102 |   }

177 |   FlowScope flowThrough(Node n, FlowScope input) {

181 |       return input;

185 |     output = traverse(n, output);

2832 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2833 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
2834 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2835 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2836 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.

===== M0587 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3035-3063
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3035 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3036 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3037 |     if (info == null) {
3038 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3039 |       if (parent == null) {
3040 |         return null;
3041 |       }
3042 | 
3043 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3044 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3045 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3046 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3047 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3048 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3049 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3050 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3051 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3052 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3053 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3054 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3055 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3056 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3057 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3058 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3059 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3060 |       }
3061 |     }
3062 |     return info;
3063 |   }

===== M0592 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0594 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 492-505
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
492 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
493 |       Node ret;
494 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
495 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
496 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
497 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
498 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
499 |       } else {
500 |         ret = transform(n);
501 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
502 |       }
503 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
504 |       return ret;
505 |     }

===== M0608 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getMinArguments()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 289-302
Documentation:
/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */
Method code:
289 |   public int getMinArguments() {
290 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
291 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position
292 |     // of the last required parameter.
293 |     int i = 0;
294 |     int min = 0;
295 |     for (Node n : getParameters()) {
296 |       i++;
297 |       if (!n.isOptionalArg() && !n.isVarArgs()) {
298 |         min = i;
299 |       }
300 |     }
301 |     return min;
302 |   }

467 |         deferred.resolve(scope);
468 |       }
469 | 
470 |       // Resolve types and attach them to scope slots.
471 |       Iterator<Var> vars = scope.getVars();
472 |       while (vars.hasNext()) {
473 |         vars.next().resolveType(typeParsingErrorReporter);
474 |       }
475 | 
476 |       // Tell the type registry that any remaining types
477 |       // are unknown.
478 |       typeRegistry.resolveTypesInScope(scope);
479 |     }

===== M0641 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.detectImplicitPrototypeCycle()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 177-197
Documentation:
/**
   * Detects a cycle in the implicit prototype chain. This method accesses
   * the {@link #getImplicitPrototype()} method and must therefore be
   * invoked only after the object is sufficiently initialized to respond to
   * calls to this method.<p>
   *
   * @return True iff an implicit prototype cycle was detected.
   */
Method code:
177 |   final boolean detectImplicitPrototypeCycle() {
178 |     // detecting cycle
179 |     this.visited = true;
180 |     ObjectType p = getImplicitPrototype();
181 |     while (p != null) {
182 |       if (p.visited) {
183 |         return true;
184 |       } else {
185 |         p.visited = true;
186 |       }
187 |       p = p.getImplicitPrototype();
188 |     }
189 | 
190 |     // clean up
191 |     p = this;
192 |     do {
193 |       p.visited = false;
194 |       p = p.getImplicitPrototype();
195 |     } while (p != null);
196 |     return false;
197 |   }

/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1520 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1521 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1522 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1523 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1524 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1525 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1526 | 
1527 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1528 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1529 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1530 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1531 | 
1532 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1533 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1534 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1535 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1536 |         }
1537 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1538 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1539 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1540 | 
1541 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1542 |         return createOptionalType(
1543 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1544 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1545 | 
1546 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1547 |         return createOptionalType(
1548 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1549 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1550 | 
1551 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1552 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1553 | 
1554 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1555 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1556 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1557 | 
1558 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1559 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1560 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1561 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1562 |           builder.addAlternate(
1563 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1564 |         }
1565 |         return builder.build();
1566 | 
1567 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1568 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1569 | 
1570 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1571 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1572 | 
1573 |       case Token.STRING:
1574 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1575 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1576 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1577 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1578 |         }
1579 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1580 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1581 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1582 |           if (typeList != null &&
1583 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1584 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1585 |             JSType parameterType =
1586 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1587 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1588 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1589 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1590 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1591 |               JSType indexType =
1592 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1593 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1594 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1595 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1596 |             }
1597 |           }
1598 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1599 |         } else {
1600 |           return namedType;
1601 |         }
1602 | 
1603 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1604 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1605 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1606 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1607 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1608 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1609 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1610 |           thisType =
1611 |               ObjectType.cast(
1612 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1613 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1614 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1615 |           if (thisType == null) {
1616 |             reporter.warning(
1617 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1618 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1619 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1620 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1621 |                 sourceName,
1622 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1623 |           }
1624 | 
1625 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1626 |           current = current.getNext();
1627 |         }
1628 | 
1629 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1630 | 
1631 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1632 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1633 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1634 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1635 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1636 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1637 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1638 |               } else {
1639 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1640 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1641 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1642 |               }
1643 |             } else {
1644 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1645 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1646 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1647 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1648 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1649 |                   reporter.warning(
1650 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1651 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1652 |                 }
1653 |               } else {
1654 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1655 |               }
1656 |             }
1657 |           }
1658 |           current = current.getNext();
1659 |         }
1660 | 
1661 |         JSType returnType =
1662 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1663 | 
1664 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1665 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1666 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1667 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1668 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1669 |             .build();
1670 |     }
1671 | 
1672 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1673 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1674 |   }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is inferred.
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the inferred definition of
   *        property that might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.
   */
Method code:
328 |   public final boolean defineInferredProperty(String propertyName,
329 |       JSType type, Node propertyNode) {
330 |     StaticSlot<JSType> originalSlot = getSlot(propertyName);
331 |     if (hasProperty(propertyName)) {
332 |       if (isPropertyTypeDeclared(propertyName)) {
333 |         // We never want to hide a declared property with an inferred property.
334 |         return true;
335 |       }
336 |       JSType originalType = getPropertyType(propertyName);
337 |       type = originalType == null ? type :
338 |           originalType.getLeastSupertype(type);
339 |     }
340 |

342 |         propertyNode);
343 |

346 |     // object can affect subtyping, it's slightly more efficient
347 |     // to register this after defining the property.
348 |     registry.registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, this);
349 | 
350 |     return result;
351 |   }

===== M0676 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo,AstNode,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 289-341
Documentation:

Method code:
289 |   private void validateTypeAnnotations(
290 |       JSDocInfo info, AstNode node, Node irNode) {
291 |     if (info.hasType()) {
292 |       boolean valid = false;
293 |       switch (node.getType()) {
294 |         // Casts are valid
295 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.LP:
296 |           valid = node instanceof ParenthesizedExpression;
297 |           break;
298 |         // Variable declarations are valid
299 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.VAR:
300 |           valid = true;
301 |           break;
302 |         // Function declarations are valid
303 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.FUNCTION:
304 |           FunctionNode fnNode = (FunctionNode)node;
305 |           valid = fnNode.getFunctionType() == FunctionNode.FUNCTION_STATEMENT;
306 |           break;
307 |         // Object literal properties and catch declarations are valid.
308 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NAME:
309 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty
310 |               || node.getParent() instanceof CatchClause
311 |               || node.getParent() instanceof FunctionNode;
312 |           break;
313 |         // Object literal properties are valid
314 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GET:
315 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.SET:
316 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NUMBER:
317 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.STRING:
318 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty;
319 |           break;
320 | 
321 |         // Property assignments are valid, if at the root of an expression.
322 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.ASSIGN:
323 |           if (node instanceof Assignment) {
324 |             valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent())
325 |                 && isPropAccess(((Assignment)node).getLeft());
326 |           }
327 |           break;
328 | 
329 |         // Property definitions are valid, if at the root of an expression.
330 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETPROP:
331 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETELEM:
332 |           valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent());
333 |           break;
334 |       }
335 |       if (!valid) {
336 |         errorReporter.warning(MISPLACED_TYPE_ANNOTATION,
337 |             sourceName,
338 |             node.getLineno(), "", 0);
339 |       }
340 |     }
341 |   }

===== M0683 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is

1252 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {

===== M0689 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0701 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 258-308
Documentation:

Method code:
258 |   @Override
259 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
260 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
261 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
262 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
263 |         return true;
264 |       }
265 | 
266 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
267 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
268 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate through all
269 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
270 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
271 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
272 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
273 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
274 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
275 |         return false;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
279 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
280 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
281 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
282 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
283 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
284 |             return false;
285 |           }
286 |         }
287 | 
288 |         return true;
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
292 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
293 | 
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
299 |       }
300 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
301 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
302 |           return false;
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |       return true;
306 |     }
307 |     return false;
308 |   }

===== M0703 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 512-569
Documentation:

Method code:
512 |     @Override
513 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
514 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
515 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
516 | 
517 |       switch (n.getType()) {
518 |         case Token.CALL:
519 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
520 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
521 |           break;
522 | 
523 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
524 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
525 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
526 |           }
527 | 
528 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
529 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
530 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n);
531 |           }
532 |           break;
533 | 
534 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
535 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
536 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
537 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
538 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
539 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
540 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
541 |           }
542 |           break;
543 | 
544 |         case Token.CATCH:
545 |           defineCatch(n);
546 |           break;
547 | 
548 |         case Token.VAR:
549 |           defineVar(n);
550 |           break;
551 | 
552 |         case Token.GETPROP:
553 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
554 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
555 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
556 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
557 |           }
558 |           break;
559 |       }
560 | 
561 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
562 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
563 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
564 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
565 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
566 |         }
567 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
568 |       }
569 |     }

   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
197 |   @Override
198 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
199 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
200 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
201 |     // show up in the type registry.
202 |     Scope newScope = null;
203 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
204 |     if (parent == null) {
205 |       JSType globalThis =
206 |           typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
207 | 
208 |       // Mark the main root, the externs root, and the src root
209 |       // with the global this type.
210 |       root.setJSType(globalThis);
211 |       root.getFirstChild().setJSType(globalThis);
212 |       root.getLastChild().setJSType(globalThis);
213 | 
214 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
215 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
216 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
217 | 
218 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
219 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
220 | 
221 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
222 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
223 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
224 |     } else {
225 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
226 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
227 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
228 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
229 |     }
230 | 
231 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
232 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
233 | 
234 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
235 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
236 |     // build properties on.
237 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
238 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
239 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
240 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
241 |         JSType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
242 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
243 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
244 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
245 |         }
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (parent == null) {
250 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
251 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
252 |           delegateCallingConventions);
253 |     }
254 |     return newScope;
255 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

     * make that a declared type (even if there's no doc info).

===== M0741 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 580-628
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
580 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
581 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
582 |     Node obj = getprop.getFirstChild();
583 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(obj);
584 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
585 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
586 |     if (objectType == null) {
587 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
588 |     } else {
589 |       // Don't add the property to @struct objects outside a constructor
590 |       if (nodeType.isStruct() && !objectType.hasProperty(propName)) {
591 |         if (!(obj.isThis() &&
592 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor())) {
593 |           return;
594 |         }
595 |       }
596 | 
597 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
598 |         return;
599 |       }
600 | 
601 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
602 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
603 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
604 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the property.
605 |         //
606 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
607 |         //    inferred type.
608 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
609 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
610 |         //    define it.
611 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
612 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
613 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
614 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) || !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
615 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
616 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, rightType, getprop);
617 |           } else {
618 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, getprop);
619 |           }
620 |         } else if (obj.isThis() &&
621 |                    getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
622 |           objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, getprop);
623 |         } else {
624 |           registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
625 |         }
626 |       }
627 |     }
628 |   }

===== M0746 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {

===== M0756 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 1005-1045
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
1005 |   @Override
1006 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1007 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
1008 |       return true;
1009 |     }
1010 | 
1011 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
1012 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
1013 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
1014 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
1015 |         return true;
1016 |       }
1017 |       if (isInterface()) {
1018 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
1019 |         return false;
1020 |       }
1021 | 
1022 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
1023 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
1024 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
1025 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
1026 |       // this as an error. Let's punt on all this for now.
1027 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
1028 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
1029 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
1030 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
1031 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
1032 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
1033 |         (other.typeOfThis.toObjectType() != null &&
1034 |              other.typeOfThis.toObjectType().getConstructor() != null &&
1035 |              other.typeOfThis.toObjectType().getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
1036 | 
1037 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
1038 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
1039 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
1040 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
1041 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
1042 |     }
1043 | 
1044 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
1045 |   }

===== M0757 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.CanCastToVisitor.castCastToHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/CanCastToVisitor.java
Original range: 95-120
Documentation:

Method code:
95 |   Boolean castCastToHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
96 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()
97 |         || thatType.isAllType()
98 |         || thatType.isNoObjectType() // TODO(johnlenz): restrict to objects
99 |         || thatType.isNoType()) {
100 |       return true;
101 |     } else if (thisType.isRecordType() || thatType.isRecordType()) {
102 |       return true;  // TODO(johnlenz): are there any misuses we can catch?
103 |     } else if (isInterface(thisType) || isInterface(thatType)) {
104 |       return true;  // TODO(johnlenz): are there any misuses we can catch?
105 |     } else if (thatType.isEnumElementType()) {
106 |       return thisType.visit(this,
107 |           thatType.toMaybeEnumElementType().getPrimitiveType());
108 |     } else if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
109 |       return canCastToUnion(thisType, thatType.toMaybeUnionType());
110 |     } else if (thatType.isFunctionType()) {
111 |       return canCastToFunction(thisType, thatType.toMaybeFunctionType());
112 |     } else if (thatType.isParameterizedType()) {
113 |       // TODO(johnlenz): once the templated type work is finished,
114 |       // restrict the type parameters.
115 |       return thisType.visit(this,
116 |           thatType.toMaybeParameterizedType().getReferencedTypeInternal());
117 |     }
118 | 
119 |     return thisType.isSubtype(thatType) || thatType.isSubtype(thisType);
120 |   }

/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.

Method code:
1367 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1368 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()

===== M0763 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 709-791
Documentation:

Method code:
709 |     @Override
710 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
711 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
712 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
713 |       if (name == null) {
714 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
715 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
716 |           errorReporter.error(
717 |             "unnamed function statement",
718 |             sourceName,
719 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
720 | 
721 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
722 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
723 |         }
724 |         name = new Name();
725 |         name.setIdentifier("");
726 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
727 |       }
728 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
729 |       Node newName = transform(name);
730 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
731 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
732 |         // declaration.
733 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
734 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
735 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
736 |         // same line as the function keyword.
737 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
738 |             functionNode.getLp();
739 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
740 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
741 |       }
742 | 
743 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
744 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
745 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
746 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
747 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
748 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
749 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
750 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
751 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
752 |       if (fnName != null) {
753 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
754 |       } else {
755 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
756 |       }
757 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
758 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
759 | 
760 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
761 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
762 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
763 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
764 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
765 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
766 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
767 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
768 |         } else {
769 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
770 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
771 |           // (an error has already been reported).
772 |           Preconditions.checkState(
773 |               config.isIdeMode
774 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
775 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
776 |         }
777 |       }
778 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
779 | 
780 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
781 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
782 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
783 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
784 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
785 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
786 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
787 |       }
788 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
789 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
790 |      return node;
791 |     }
