===== M0773 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 692-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.ON_EX, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0778 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:

===== M0790 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 256-282
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
256 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
257 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
258 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
259 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
260 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
261 | 
262 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
263 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
264 |       // if (this.x == null) {
265 |       //   this.initializeX();
266 |       //   this.x.foo();
267 |       // }
268 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
269 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
270 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
271 |       //
272 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
273 |       if (n.isGetProp() &&
274 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
275 |         return true;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
279 |       return false;
280 |     }
281 |     return true;
282 |   }

===== M0792 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1722-1763
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1722 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1723 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1724 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1725 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1726 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1727 |       // {?} - right curly
1728 |       // {?=} - equals
1729 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1730 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1731 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1732 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1733 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1734 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1735 |       token = next();
1736 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1737 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1738 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1739 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1740 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1741 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1742 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1743 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1744 |       }
1745 | 
1746 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1747 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1748 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1749 |     } else {
1750 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1751 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1752 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1753 |           next();
1754 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1755 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1756 |           next();
1757 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1758 |         }
1759 |       }
1760 | 
1761 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1762 |     }
1763 |   }

===== M0794 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 172-259
Documentation:

Method code:
172 |   @Override
173 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
174 |     switch (n.getType()) {
175 |       case Token.CALL:
176 |         boolean isExpr = parent.isExprResult();
177 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
178 |         if (left.isGetProp()) {
179 |           Node name = left.getFirstChild();
180 |           if (name.isName() &&
181 |               GOOG.equals(name.getString())) {
182 |             // For the sake of simplicity, we report code changes
183 |             // when we see a provides/requires, and don't worry about
184 |             // reporting the change when we actually do the replacement.
185 |             String methodName = name.getNext().getString();
186 |             if ("base".equals(methodName)) {
187 |               processBaseClassCall(t, n);
188 |             } else if (!isExpr) {
189 |               // All other methods must be called in an EXPR.
190 |               break;
191 |             } else if ("require".equals(methodName)) {
192 |               processRequireCall(t, n, parent);
193 |             } else if ("provide".equals(methodName)) {
194 |               processProvideCall(t, n, parent);
195 |             } else if ("exportSymbol".equals(methodName)) {
196 |               Node arg = left.getNext();
197 |               if (arg.isString()) {
198 |                 int dot = arg.getString().indexOf('.');
199 |                 if (dot == -1) {
200 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString());
201 |                 } else {
202 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString().substring(0, dot));
203 |                 }
204 |               }
205 |             } else if ("addDependency".equals(methodName)) {
206 |               CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
207 |               List<String> typeDecls =
208 |                   convention.identifyTypeDeclarationCall(n);
209 |               if (typeDecls != null) {
210 |                 for (String typeDecl : typeDecls) {
211 |                   compiler.getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(typeDecl);
212 |                 }
213 |               }
214 | 
215 |               // We can't modify parent, so just create a node that will
216 |               // get compiled out.
217 |               parent.replaceChild(n, IR.number(0));
218 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
219 |             } else if ("setCssNameMapping".equals(methodName)) {
220 |               processSetCssNameMapping(t, n, parent);
221 |             }
222 |           }
223 |         }
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
227 |       case Token.NAME:
228 |         // If this is an assignment to a provided name, remove the provided
229 |         // object.
230 |         handleCandidateProvideDefinition(t, n, parent);
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
234 |         handleTypedefDefinition(t, n, parent);
235 |         break;
236 | 
237 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
238 |         // If this is a declaration of a provided named function, this is an
239 |         // error. Hoisted functions will explode if they're provided.
240 |         if (t.inGlobalScope() &&
241 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
242 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
243 |           ProvidedName pn = providedNames.get(name);
244 |           if (pn != null) {
245 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, FUNCTION_NAMESPACE_ERROR, name));
246 |           }
247 |         }
248 |         break;
249 | 
250 |       case Token.GETPROP:
251 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isName() &&
252 |             !parent.isCall() &&
253 |             !parent.isAssign() &&
254 |             "goog.base".equals(n.getQualifiedName())) {
255 |           reportBadBaseClassUse(t, n, "May only be called directly.");
256 |         }
257 |         break;
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0798 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 189-301
Documentation:

Method code:
189 |   @Override
190 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
191 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
192 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
193 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
194 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
195 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
196 |     // worthwhile.
197 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
198 |     Node condition = null;
199 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
200 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
201 | 
202 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
203 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
204 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
205 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
206 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
207 | 
208 |       switch (branch) {
209 |         case ON_TRUE:
210 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
211 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
212 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
213 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
214 | 
215 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
216 | 
217 |             if (item.isVar()) {
218 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
219 |             }
220 |             if (item.isName()) {
221 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
222 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
223 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
224 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
225 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
226 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
227 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
228 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
229 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
230 |                 }
231 |               }
232 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
233 |             }
234 |             newScope = informed;
235 |             break;
236 |           }
237 | 
238 |           // FALL THROUGH
239 | 
240 |         case ON_FALSE:
241 |           if (condition == null) {
242 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
243 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
244 |               condition = source;
245 | 
246 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
247 |               // of the loop.
248 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
249 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
250 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
251 |               }
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (condition != null) {
256 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
257 |                 condition.isOr()) {
258 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
259 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
260 |               // scope on false.
261 |               //
262 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
263 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
264 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
265 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
266 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
267 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
268 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
269 | 
270 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
271 |               // of the loop.
272 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
273 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
274 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
275 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
276 |               }
277 |               newScope =
278 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
279 |                       condition,
280 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
281 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
282 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
283 |             } else {
284 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
285 |               // of the loop.
286 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
287 |                 conditionFlowScope =
288 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
289 |               }
290 |               newScope =
291 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
292 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
293 |             }
294 |           }
295 |           break;
296 |       }
297 | 
298 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
299 |     }
300 |     return result;
301 |   }

===== M0824 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 2024-2075
Documentation:

Method code:
2024 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
2025 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
2026 |         return;
2027 |       }
2028 | 
2029 |       if (n.isReturn() && n.getFirstChild() != null) {
2030 |         data.get(t.getScopeRoot()).recordNonEmptyReturn();
2031 |       }
2032 | 
2033 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() <= 1) {
2034 |         // The first-order function analyzer looks at two types of variables:
2035 |         //
2036 |         // 1) Local variables that are assigned in inner scopes ("escaped vars")
2037 |         //
2038 |         // 2) Local variables that are assigned more than once.
2039 |         //
2040 |         // We treat all global variables as escaped by default, so there's
2041 |         // no reason to do this extra computation for them.
2042 |         return;
2043 |       }
2044 | 
2045 |       if (n.isName() && NodeUtil.isLValue(n) &&
2046 |           // Be careful of bleeding functions, which create variables
2047 |           // in the inner scope, not the scope where the name appears.
2048 |           !NodeUtil.isBleedingFunctionName(n)) {
2049 |         String name = n.getString();
2050 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
2051 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
2052 |         if (var != null) {
2053 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
2054 |           if (ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2055 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordAssignedName(name);
2056 |           }
2057 | 
2058 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2059 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordEscapedVarName(name);
2060 |           }
2061 |         }
2062 |       } else if (n.isGetProp() && n.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
2063 |           NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
2064 |         String name = NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(n).getString();
2065 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
2066 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
2067 |         if (var != null) {
2068 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
2069 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2070 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode())
2071 |                 .recordEscapedQualifiedName(n.getQualifiedName());
2072 |           }
2073 |         }
2074 |       }
2075 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.resolve(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)

Original range: 1419-1432
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
1419 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1420 |     if (resolved) {
1421 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
1422 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
1423 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
1424 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1425 |       }
1426 |       return resolveResult;
1427 |     }
1428 |     resolved = true;
1429 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
1430 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
1431 |     return resolveResult;
1432 |   }

===== M0841 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

===== M0842 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.JSType(JSTypeRegistry,ImmutableList<String>,ImmutableList<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 114-144
Documentation:

Method code:
114 |   JSType(JSTypeRegistry registry, ImmutableList<String> templateKeys,
115 |       ImmutableList<JSType> templatizedTypes) {
116 |     this.registry = registry;
117 | 
118 |     // Do sanity checking on the specified keys and templatized types.
119 |     int keysLength = templateKeys == null ? 0 : templateKeys.size();
120 |     int typesLength = templatizedTypes == null ? 0 : templatizedTypes.size();
121 |     if (typesLength > keysLength) {
122 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(
123 |           "Cannot have more templatized types than template keys");
124 |     } else if (typesLength < keysLength) {
125 |       // If there are fewer templatized types than keys, extend the templatized
126 |       // types list to match the number of keys, using UNKNOWN_TYPE for the
127 |       // unspecified types.
128 |       ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
129 |       if (typesLength > 0) {
130 |         builder.addAll(templatizedTypes);
131 |       }
132 |       for (int i = 0; i < keysLength - typesLength; i++) {
133 |         builder.add(registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE));
134 |       }
135 |       templatizedTypes = builder.build();
136 |     } else if (keysLength == 0 && typesLength == 0) {
137 |       // Ensure that both lists are non-null.
138 |       templateKeys = ImmutableList.of();
139 |       templatizedTypes = ImmutableList.of();
140 |     }
141 | 
142 |     this.templateKeys = templateKeys;
143 |     this.templatizedTypes = templatizedTypes;
144 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 160-181
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
160 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
161 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
162 |     String methodName = null;
163 |     if (callName.isGetProp()) {
164 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
165 |     } else if (callName.isName()) {
166 |       String name = callName.getString();
167 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
168 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
169 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
170 |       }
171 |     }
172 | 
173 |     if (methodName != null) {
174 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
175 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
176 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
177 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
178 |       }
179 |     }
180 |     return null;
181 |   }

===== M0859 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 553-568
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
553 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
554 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
555 |       Set<String> directives = null;
556 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
557 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
558 |         if (directives == null) {
559 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
560 |         } else {
561 |           directives.add(directive);
562 |         }
563 |       }
564 | 
565 |       if (directives != null) {
566 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
567 |       }
568 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1332-1334
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1332 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1333 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1334 |   }

===== M0883 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 159-205
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
159 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
160 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
161 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
162 |       case Token.BLOCK:
163 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
164 |       case Token.TRY:
165 |         return true;
166 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
167 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
168 |         // bleeds into the local scope and parameters are assigned
169 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
170 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
171 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
172 |         // control is going into the function's body, represented by the second
173 |         // child.
174 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
175 |       case Token.WHILE:
176 |       case Token.DO:
177 |       case Token.IF:
178 |         // These control structures are represented by a node that holds the
179 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
180 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.FOR:
183 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structures in that
184 |         // it has an initialization and an increment statement. Those
185 |         // two statements have corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
186 |         // The FOR node only represents the condition check for each iteration.
187 |         // That way the following:
188 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
189 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
190 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
191 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
192 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
193 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |         } else {
195 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
196 |         }
197 |       case Token.SWITCH:
198 |       case Token.CASE:
199 |       case Token.CATCH:
200 |       case Token.WITH:
201 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
202 |       default:
203 |         return false;
204 |     }
205 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1487-1516
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1487 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1488 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1489 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1490 |     // merge to fix this.
1491 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1492 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1493 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1494 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1495 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1496 | 
1497 |       if (value != null) {
1498 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1499 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1500 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1501 | 
1502 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1503 |         if (info == null) {
1504 |           info = varInfo;
1505 |         }
1506 | 
1507 |         checkEnumAlias(t, info, value);
1508 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1509 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1510 |         } else {
1511 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1512 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1513 |         }
1514 |       }
1515 |     }
1516 |   }

===== M0899 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
274 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
275 |     this.options = options;
276 |     if (errorManager == null) {
277 |       if (outStream == null) {
278 |         setErrorManager(
279 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
280 |       } else {
281 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
282 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
283 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
284 |         setErrorManager(printer);
285 |       }
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
289 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
290 |       options.checkTypes = true;
291 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
292 |       options.checkTypes = false;
293 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
294 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
295 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
296 |       // parser type warnings.
297 |       options.setWarningLevel(
298 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
299 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
300 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
304 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
305 |       options.setWarningLevel(
306 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
307 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
308 |     }
309 | 
310 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
311 |       options.setWarningLevel(
312 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
313 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
314 |     }
315 | 
316 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
317 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
318 |     guards.add(
319 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
320 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
321 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
322 | 
323 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
324 | 
325 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
326 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
327 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
328 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
329 |     // error off.
330 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
331 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
332 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
333 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
337 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.canPropertyBeDefined(JSType,String)

Documentation:
/**
   * Returns whether the given property can possibly be set on the given type.
   */
Method code:
697 |   public boolean canPropertyBeDefined(JSType type, String propertyName) {
698 |     if (typesIndexedByProperty.containsKey(propertyName)) {
699 |       for (JSType alt :
700 |                typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName).getAlternates()) {
701 |         JSType greatestSubtype = alt.getGreatestSubtype(type);
702 |         if (!greatestSubtype.isEmptyType()) {
703 |           // We've found a type with this property. Now we just have to make
704 |           // sure it's not a type used for internal bookkeeping.
705 |           RecordType maybeRecordType = greatestSubtype.toMaybeRecordType();
706 |           if (maybeRecordType != null && maybeRecordType.isSynthetic()) {
707 |             continue;
708 |           }
709 | 
710 |           return true;
711 |         }
712 |       }
713 |     }
714 |     return false;
715 |   }

===== M0918 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 654-742
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
654 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
655 |     if (this == that) {
656 |       return true;
657 |     }
658 | 
659 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
660 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
661 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
662 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
663 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
664 |         // with everyone.
665 |         return true;
666 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
667 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
668 |         // both unknown.
669 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
670 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
671 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
672 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
673 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
674 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
675 |         return false;
676 |       }
677 |     }
678 | 
679 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
680 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
681 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
682 |     }
683 | 
684 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
685 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
686 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
687 |     }
688 | 
689 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
690 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
691 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
692 |     }
693 | 
694 |     ParameterizedType thisParamType = toMaybeParameterizedType();
695 |     ParameterizedType thatParamType = that.toMaybeParameterizedType();
696 |     if (thisParamType != null || thatParamType != null) {
697 |       // Check if one type is parameterized, but the other is not.
698 |       boolean paramsMatch = false;
699 |       if (thisParamType != null && thatParamType != null) {
700 |         paramsMatch = thisParamType.getParameterType().checkEquivalenceHelper(
701 |             thatParamType.getParameterType(), eqMethod);
702 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY) {
703 |         paramsMatch = false;
704 |       } else {
705 |         // If one of the type parameters is unknown, but the other is not,
706 |         // then we consider these the same for the purposes of data flow
707 |         // and invariance.
708 |         paramsMatch = true;
709 |       }
710 | 
711 |       JSType thisRootType = thisParamType == null ?
712 |           this : thisParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
713 |       JSType thatRootType = thatParamType == null ?
714 |           that : thatParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
715 |       return paramsMatch &&
716 |           thisRootType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatRootType, eqMethod);
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
720 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
721 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
722 |     }
723 | 
724 |     // Unbox other proxies.
725 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
726 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
727 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
728 |               that, eqMethod);
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
732 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
733 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
734 |           eqMethod);
735 |     }
736 | 
737 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
738 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
739 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
740 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
741 |     return this == that;
742 |   }

===== M0928 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 433-458

Method code:
433 |   @Override
434 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
435 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
436 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
437 |     switch (n.getType()) {
438 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
439 |         // normal type checking
440 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
441 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
442 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
443 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
444 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
445 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
446 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
447 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
448 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
449 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
450 |         }
451 | 
452 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
453 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
454 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
455 |         break;
456 |     }
457 |     return true;
458 |   }

===== M0934 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2038-2053
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as syntactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2038 |   public static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2039 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2040 |         n.isGetElem());
2041 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2042 |     if (parent == null) {
2043 |       return false;
2044 |     }
2045 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2046 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2047 |         || parent.isVar()
2048 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2049 |         || parent.isDec()
2050 |         || parent.isInc()
2051 |         || parent.isParamList()
2052 |         || parent.isCatch();
2053 |   }

Method code:
183 |   @Override
184 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> enclosing) {
185 |     // TODO(user): Investigate whether it is really necessary to keep two
186 |     // different mechanisms for resolving named types, and if so, which order
187 |     // makes more sense. Now, resolution via registry is first in order to
188 |     // avoid triggering the warnings built into the resolution via properties.
189 |     boolean resolved = resolveViaRegistry(t, enclosing);
190 |     if (detectImplicitPrototypeCycle()) {
191 |       handleTypeCycle(t);
192 |     }
193 | 
194 |     if (resolved) {
195 |       super.resolveInternal(t, enclosing);
196 |       finishPropertyContinuations();
197 |       return registry.isLastGeneration() ?
198 |           getReferencedType() : this;
199 |     }
200 | 
201 |     resolveViaProperties(t, enclosing);
202 |     if (detectImplicitPrototypeCycle()) {
203 |       handleTypeCycle(t);
204 |     }
205 | 
206 |     super.resolveInternal(t, enclosing);
207 |     if (isResolved()) {
208 |       finishPropertyContinuations();
209 |     }
210 |     return registry.isLastGeneration() ?
211 |         getReferencedType() : this;
212 |   }

===== M0947 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                                   String sourceString,
106 |                                   Config config,
107 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
147 |   }

===== M0951 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1684-1713
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub declarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1684 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1685 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1686 |         Node n = stub.node;
1687 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1688 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1689 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1690 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1691 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1692 | 
1693 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1694 |           continue;
1695 |         }
1696 | 
1697 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1698 |         // in the type registry.
1699 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1700 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1701 | 
1702 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1703 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1704 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1705 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1706 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1707 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1708 |         } else {
1709 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1710 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1711 |         }
1712 |       }
1713 |     }

463 | 
464 |       for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> predNode : predNodes) {
465 |         BranchedFlowState<L> predNodeState = predNode.getAnnotation();
466 | 
467 |         L in = predNodeState.out.get(
468 |             getCfg().getDirectedSuccNodes(predNode).indexOf(node));
469 | 
470 |         values.add(in);
471 |       }
472 |       if (getCfg().getEntry() == node) {
473 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
474 |       } else if (!values.isEmpty()) {
475 |         state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
476 |       }
477 |     }

===== M0977 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 317-364
Documentation:

Method code:
317 |   @Override
318 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
319 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
320 |       return true;
321 |     }
322 | 
323 |     // Union types
324 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
325 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
326 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
327 |       return false;
328 |     }
329 | 
330 |     // record types
331 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
332 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     // Interfaces
336 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
337 |     // to the interface instance.
338 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
339 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
340 | 
341 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
342 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
343 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
344 |           return true;
345 |         }
346 |       }
347 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
348 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
349 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
350 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
351 |           return true;
352 |         }
353 |       }
354 |     }
355 | 
356 |     // other prototype based objects
357 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
358 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
359 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
360 |       // to avoid guessing.
361 |       return true;
362 |     }
363 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
364 |   }

===== M0978 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.registerMismatch(JSType,JSType,JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 655-679
Documentation:

Method code:
655 |   private void registerMismatch(JSType found, JSType required, JSError error) {
656 |     // Don't register a mismatch for differences in null or undefined or if the
657 |     // code didn't downcast.
658 |     found = found.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
659 |     required = required.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
660 |     if (found.isSubtype(required) || required.isSubtype(found)) {
661 |       return;
662 |     }
663 | 
664 |     mismatches.add(new TypeMismatch(found, required, error));
665 |     if (found.isFunctionType() &&
666 |         required.isFunctionType()) {
667 |       FunctionType fnTypeA = found.toMaybeFunctionType();
668 |       FunctionType fnTypeB = required.toMaybeFunctionType();
669 |       Iterator<Node> paramItA = fnTypeA.getParameters().iterator();
670 |       Iterator<Node> paramItB = fnTypeB.getParameters().iterator();
671 |       while (paramItA.hasNext() && paramItB.hasNext()) {
672 |         registerIfMismatch(paramItA.next().getJSType(),
673 |             paramItB.next().getJSType(), error);
674 |       }
675 | 
676 |       registerIfMismatch(
677 |           fnTypeA.getReturnType(), fnTypeB.getReturnType(), error);
678 |     }
679 |   }

/**
     * Declares all of a function's arguments.
     */
Method code:
1945 |     private void declareArguments(Node functionNode) {
1946 |       Node astParameters = functionNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
1947 |       Node iifeArgumentNode = null;
1948 | 
1949 |       if (NodeUtil.isCallOrNewTarget(functionNode)) {
1950 |         iifeArgumentNode = functionNode.getNext();
1951 |       }
1952 | 
1953 |       Node body = astParameters.getNext();
1954 |       FunctionType functionType =
1955 |           JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(functionNode.getJSType());
1956 |       if (functionType != null) {
1957 |         Node jsDocParameters = functionType.getParametersNode();
1958 |         if (jsDocParameters != null) {
1959 |           Node jsDocParameter = jsDocParameters.getFirstChild();
1960 |           for (Node astParameter : astParameters.children()) {
1961 |             JSType paramType = jsDocParameter == null ?
1962 |                 unknownType : jsDocParameter.getJSType();
1963 |             boolean inferred = paramType == null || paramType == unknownType;
1964 | 
1965 |             if (iifeArgumentNode != null && inferred) {
1966 |               String argumentName = iifeArgumentNode.getQualifiedName();
1967 |               Var argumentVar =
1968 |                   argumentName == null || scope.getParent() == null
1969 |                   ? null : scope.getParent().getVar(argumentName);
1970 |               if (argumentVar != null && !argumentVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1971 |                 paramType = argumentVar.getType();
1972 |               }
1973 |             }
1974 | 
1975 |             if (paramType == null) {
1976 |               paramType = unknownType;
1977 |             }
1978 | 
1979 |             defineSlot(astParameter, functionNode, paramType, inferred);
1980 | 
1981 |             if (jsDocParameter != null) {
1982 |               jsDocParameter = jsDocParameter.getNext();
1983 |             }
1984 |             if (iifeArgumentNode != null) {
1985 |               iifeArgumentNode = iifeArgumentNode.getNext();
1986 |             }
1987 |           }
1988 |         }
1989 |       }
1990 |     } // end declareArguments

/**
   * Determines whether this node is testing for the existence of a property.
   * If true, we will not emit warnings about a missing property.
   *
   * @param getProp The GETPROP being tested.

1439 |   private boolean isPropertyTest(Node getProp) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 427-495
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
427 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
428 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
429 |     if (argsParent == null) {
430 |       if (info == null) {
431 |         return this;
432 |       } else {
433 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
434 |       }
435 |     }
436 | 
437 |     // arguments
438 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
439 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
440 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
441 |     }
442 | 
443 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
444 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
445 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
446 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
447 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
448 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
449 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
450 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
451 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
452 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
453 | 
454 |       // type from JSDocInfo
455 |       JSType parameterType = null;
456 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
457 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
458 | 
459 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
460 |         parameterType =
461 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
462 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
463 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
464 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
465 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
466 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
467 |       } else {
468 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
472 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
473 |           isOptionalParam,
474 |           isVarArgs);
475 | 
476 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
477 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
478 |       }
479 |     }
480 | 
481 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
482 |     if (!isVarArgs) {
483 |       while (oldParameterType != null && !isVarArgs) {
484 |         builder.newParameterFromNode(oldParameterType);
485 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
486 |       }
487 |     }
488 | 
489 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
490 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
494 |     return this;
495 |   }

===== M1043 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getGreatestSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1054-1106
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common
   * getGreatestSubtype implementations.
   */
Method code:
1054 |   static JSType getGreatestSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1055 |     if (thisType.isFunctionType() && thatType.isFunctionType()) {
1056 |       // The FunctionType sub-lattice is not well-defined. i.e., the
1057 |       // proposition
1058 |       // A < B => sup(A, B) == B
1059 |       // does not hold because of unknown parameters and return types.
1060 |       // See the comment in supAndInfHelper for more info on this.
1061 |       return thisType.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(
1062 |           thatType.toMaybeFunctionType(), false);
1063 |     } else if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1064 |       return thisType;
1065 |     } else if (thisType.isUnknownType() || thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1066 |       // The greatest subtype with any unknown type is the universal
1067 |       // unknown type, unless the two types are equal.
1068 |       return thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType) ? thisType :
1069 |           thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1070 |     } else if (thisType.isUnionType()) {
1071 |       return thisType.toMaybeUnionType().meet(thatType);
1072 |     } else if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1073 |       return thatType.toMaybeUnionType().meet(thisType);
1074 |     } else if (thisType.isParameterizedType()) {
1075 |       return thisType.toMaybeParameterizedType().getGreatestSubtypeHelper(
1076 |           thatType);
1077 |     }  else if (thatType.isParameterizedType()) {
1078 |       return thatType.toMaybeParameterizedType().getGreatestSubtypeHelper(
1079 |           thisType);
1080 |     } else if (thisType.isSubtype(thatType)) {
1081 |       return filterNoResolvedType(thisType);
1082 |     } else if (thatType.isSubtype(thisType)) {
1083 |       return filterNoResolvedType(thatType);
1084 |     } else if (thisType.isRecordType()) {
1085 |       return thisType.toMaybeRecordType().getGreatestSubtypeHelper(thatType);
1086 |     } else if (thatType.isRecordType()) {
1087 |       return thatType.toMaybeRecordType().getGreatestSubtypeHelper(thisType);
1088 |     }
1089 | 
1090 |     if (thisType.isEnumElementType()) {
1091 |       JSType inf = thisType.toMaybeEnumElementType().meet(thatType);
1092 |       if (inf != null) {
1093 |         return inf;
1094 |       }
1095 |     } else if (thatType.isEnumElementType()) {
1096 |       JSType inf = thatType.toMaybeEnumElementType().meet(thisType);
1097 |       if (inf != null) {
1098 |         return inf;
1099 |       }
1100 |     }
1101 | 
1102 |     if (thisType.isObject() && thatType.isObject()) {
1103 |       return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_OBJECT_TYPE);
1104 |     }
1105 |     return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE);
1106 |   }

===== M1057 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 233-291
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
233 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
234 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
235 |     if (oldType == null) {
236 |       return this;
237 |     }
238 | 
239 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
240 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
241 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
242 |       // Not a function literal.
243 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
244 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
245 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
246 |       }
247 |     } else {
248 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
249 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
250 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
251 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
252 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
253 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
254 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
255 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
256 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
257 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
258 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
259 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
260 | 
261 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
262 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
263 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
264 | 
265 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
266 |           // arguments.
267 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
268 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
269 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
270 |           }
271 |         } else {
272 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
273 |               paramBuilder,
274 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
275 |               warnedAboutArgList,
276 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
277 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
278 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
279 |         }
280 |       }
281 | 
282 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal,
283 |       // but make them optional.
284 |       while (oldParams.hasNext()) {
285 |         paramBuilder.newOptionalParameterFromNode(oldParams.next());
286 |       }
287 | 
288 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
289 |     }
290 |     return this;
291 |   }

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.

Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {

===== M1081 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

===== M1085 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1340-1376
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1340 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1341 |     // unknown
1342 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1343 |       return true;
1344 |     }
1345 |     // all type
1346 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1347 |       return true;
1348 |     }
1349 |     // equality
1350 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1351 |       return true;
1352 |     }
1353 |     // unions
1354 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1355 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1356 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1357 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1358 |           return true;
1359 |         }
1360 |       }
1361 |       return false;
1362 |     }
1363 | 
1364 |     // parameterized types.
1365 |     if (thisType.isParameterizedType()) {
1366 |       return thisType.toMaybeParameterizedType().isParameterizeSubtypeOf(
1367 |           thatType);
1368 |     }
1369 | 
1370 |     // proxy types
1371 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1372 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1373 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1374 |     }
1375 |     return false;
1376 |   }
